# UX3 (Invite Links, Event Details, Calendar and Reminders) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give invited guests a complete, private path: invite links that open the app from Messages, an optional end time, private Meeting details, a pinned Note to guests, Add to calendar, and two reminders scheduled on their own phone.

**Architecture:**
- **One hosted-compatible migration, `20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`, built in two reviewed slices (Tasks 1–2)**, with SQL tests in full and hosted-baseline modes. Task 3 adds a rollback script, which the hosted rehearsal proves restores the exact pre-UX3 definitions and notes.
- **One small local-only migration, `20261015130000_ux3_note_delivery.sql` (Task 2)**, extends the unreleased notification sender's send-time check to the Note to guests. The worker's text gains the note and "can’t go" wording.
- **App work starts with pure, test-first helpers (Task 4)**, then the data layer (Task 5) and device services for reminders and the calendar (Task 6), then the screens (Tasks 7–9).
- **The invite-link site (Task 10)** is generated from `links/` and deployed by the owner as its own Vercel project on `link.resbite.com`.
- **Hosted rollout, the link site deployment and the TestFlight build are not tasks.** The controller and the owner do them after the final review, each live change with the owner's explicit approval (see "Rollout").

**Tech Stack:** PostgreSQL 17 (local disposable cluster via `scripts/test-database.sh`), Supabase RLS, Deno-style worker code tested with Node 22 `node:test`, Expo SDK 57, React Native 0.86 (Fabric), expo-router 57 (typed routes), react-native-web 0.21, `expo-notifications` (installed), `expo-calendar` (new), Playwright browser checks with intercepted Supabase, a static site on Vercel.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-ux3-design.md`, approved by the owner on 15 October 2026. This plan amends it in three places (decisions P1–P3), which the owner confirms with this plan.

**Domain change (15 October 2026, after the build):** the owner moved invite links from `go.resbite.com` to `link.resbite.com`. The prose and Rollout below use the new name; the embedded blocks keep the original text as built.

## Global Constraints

- **Node:**
  - Before any mobile or worker command, run `export PATH="/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/bin:$PATH"`.
  - Mobile commands run from `mobile/`. Worker tests: `node --experimental-transform-types --test supabase/functions/_shared/*.test.ts` from the repo root.
- **Expo docs.** Read https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/ before using an Expo API in a new way (`mobile/AGENTS.md`). The calendar uses `createEventInCalendarAsync` from `expo-calendar/legacy`; local reminders use `expo-notifications`.
- **Dependencies and native configuration:**
  - exactly one new dependency, `expo-calendar`, installed with `npx expo install expo-calendar`;
  - the only native configuration changes are the `expo-calendar` plugin's purpose strings and `ios.associatedDomains: ["applinks:link.resbite.com"]` (Task 6).
- **Supabase:**
  - Tasks never call the Supabase MCP tools, never connect to the hosted project and never run `supabase db push`.
  - "Database migrations each need explicit owner approval." Hosted rollout is the controller's job, after the final review.
- **Migration files:**
  - `supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql` is hosted-compatible. It may reference only objects created by the files in `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/migrations.txt`.
  - `supabase/migrations/20261015130000_ux3_note_delivery.sql` is local-only and goes live only with the notification sender, after that has its own approval.
- **SQL conventions (copy the existing style):**
  - Privileged code lives in `private` as `security definer` with `set search_path=''`, behind a `public` `security invoker` wrapper.
  - Every new function gets `revoke all … from public, anon, authenticated, service_role`, then `grant execute … to authenticated` only where testers call it.
  - Error codes: `42501` for permission or owner, `22023` for invalid input or unavailable, `40001` for conflicts.
  - Replaced functions use `create or replace` with the identical signature, which keeps the owner and grants.
- **SQL tests:**
  - Each runs inside `begin; … rollback;`, using synthetic `@resbite-test.invalid` identities. `ux3.sql` uses UUID prefixes `f1`–`f3`, and the rehearsal uses `f9`.
  - Run `./scripts/test-database.sh` (full) and `./scripts/test-database.sh --hosted-baseline supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql` from the repo root. Until the hosted rollout, hosted-baseline mode always gets this candidate argument.
- **Privacy:**
  - Meeting details reach only the organizer and guests whose answer is Going or Maybe; the database enforces it.
  - Meeting details, the place, the Note to guests text and links never appear in notification text, reminders, calendar entries or link previews.
  - Reminder text is the event name and time only.
- **Copy:**
  - Curly apostrophes (’). Strings quoted in this plan are exact.
  - Protected wording keeps its exact text, except the changes the spec lists: "Meeting details" and its lines, "Can’t go" / "Can’t go now", the Note to guests, the reminders, and the Notifications screen.
- **Kit and accessibility (UX1/UX4 rules):**
  - one short heading per screen; icon-first chips; explanations behind ⓘ; 44 pt targets;
  - Larger Text never scrolls sideways at 320 px; VoiceOver labels; Reduce Motion;
  - no Reanimated layout animations (`entering`, `exiting` or `layout`); `src/design/layoutAnimations.test.ts` guards this.
- **Device fakes.** On the web and in preview, reminder and calendar calls go to an in-memory fake mirrored on `globalThis.__resbiteDevice` (`{ reminders, calendar, permission }`), in development builds only. Browser scripts read it; release builds never create it.
- **Unit tests.** Every new mobile unit-test file is appended to the `test` chain in `mobile/package.json`; the link-site test runs as `node --import tsx ../links/generate.test.mjs` in the same chain.
- **Typed routes are on.** After changing route params, start Expo web once with `--clear` before `npm run check`.
- **Browser QA:**
  1. Start `cd mobile && CI=1 npx expo start --web --port 8081 --clear` in the background. If port 8081 is taken, use `--port 8082` and prefix every script with `RESBITE_QA_URL=http://localhost:8082`.
  2. Wait for HTTP 200, then run `node scripts/<name>.mjs` from `mobile/`.
  3. Restart with `--clear` after source edits, and stop the server when done.
  4. After closing an `InfoSheet`, wait until `getByRole("button", { name: "Close sheet" })` has count 0 before the next interaction (a closing Modal briefly leaves two).
- **Plan blocks.** Every code block that a step writes or applies verbatim has a marker line `<!-- block: NAME -->` above it in this file. Use the blocks exactly; never retype them.
  - Extract one with this shell function, run from the repo root:

````bash
plan_block() { awk -v n="$1" '$0 == "<!-- block: " n " -->" {f = 1; next} f && !p && /^```/ {p = 1; next} p && /^```$/ {exit} p' docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-ux3.md; }
````

  - A whole file: `plan_block NAME > PATH`. An addition to the end of a file: `plan_block NAME >> PATH`. A patch: `plan_block NAME | git apply --directory=mobile` (patch paths are relative to `mobile/`).
  - A patch that doesn't apply means the tree differs from what this plan expects. Stop and report it; don't edit the patch to fit.
- **Commits.** Commit at the end of each task; fix commits are allowed.
  - The message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
  - Stage only the task's files, never `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.mcp.json` or `skills-lock.json`.

## Review Focus

1. **A guest switches from Going to Can’t go:**
   - expected: Meeting details stop reaching them at once, the event page shows the locked line, and their reminders disappear on the next sync;
   - pinned by Task 1 (`ux3.sql` block 2: `respond` to `declined` hides the row), Task 4 (`reminders.test.ts`: declined gets none) and Task 8 (`verify-event.mjs`: answering Can’t go shows the locked line).
2. **An older TestFlight build edits an event that has an end time or Meeting details:**
   - expected: the end moves with the start and keeps its length, and the Meeting details are saved, not lost;
   - pinned by Task 1 (`ux3.sql` blocks 1 and 1b, `change_plan_v2` with a note and an end).
3. **The organizer moves the start while reminders are scheduled:**
   - expected: the next sync moves both reminders to the new time and keeps nothing at the old one;
   - pinned by Task 4 (`reminderChanges` moves a changed reminder) and Task 6 (`reminders.test.ts` service sync with a fake adapter).
4. **A note posted around the start ("Running 10 minutes late"):**
   - expected: allowed until the end (or an hour after the start), refused after that or on a cancelled event, and the app's sheet agrees with the database;
   - pinned by Task 2 (`ux3.sql` block 3 windows) and Task 4 (`canPostGuestNote` tests).
5. **Signing out on a shared phone:**
   - expected: every Resbite reminder, the reminder settings and the calendar records are removed, and no reminder for the old account fires later;
   - pinned by Task 6 (`sessionLifecycle.test.ts` clears all three).

## Decisions this plan makes

- **P1. Spec amendment: Add to calendar asks for no permission at all.**
  - It uses `createEventInCalendarAsync` from `expo-calendar/legacy`, which opens iPhone's own form. iPhone saves the event itself, so Resbite never gets calendar access, not even write-only (the spec said write-only).
  - The build still declares honest calendar and reminders purpose strings, because Apple rejects builds whose libraries reference those APIs without them (build 3's motion rejection).
- **P2. Spec amendment: one hosted migration plus one local-only migration.**
  - The unreleased notification sender re-checks each Note to guests notice at send time, like change notices.
  - That check lives in a local-only function, so it gets its own local-only migration, as UX2 did. Nothing local-only goes live with UX3.
- **P3. Spec amendment: the Meeting details flag.**
  - `plans.has_meeting_details` lets every invitee know Meeting details exist, so the locked line appears only when there is something to unlock. The text itself stays private.
- **P4. The database does the deletion clean-up.**
  - Account deletion's existing redaction trigger (hosted) now also removes Meeting details and the Note to guests, so the local-only deletion code needs no change.
- **P5. Old save functions delegate.**
  - `create_plan_v2` and `change_plan_v2` keep their signatures and call the v3 functions. v2 edits keep an existing end's length.
  - The first-generation `create_plan` (unused since CE1) now refuses a non-empty note, because the old note column must stay empty.
- **P6. Note to guests notification text:** "Sam posted a note to guests." It never includes the note itself.
- **P7. Reminders are capped at the 60 soonest,** under iOS's limit of 64 pending notifications.
- **P8. The link site:**
  - It serves the Resbite mark as the preview image and redirects `/` to `www.resbite.com`.
  - Its page reads "You’re invited" with one line, a button and a private-testing note.
- **P9. Reminders play the default sound** when they arrive in the background, because a reminder nobody hears isn't much of one. Inside the app they show as a banner with no sound.
- **P10. Add to calendar needs iOS 17 or later.**
  - On iOS 17+, iPhone's form opens with no permission check.
  - On older iOS the library would demand calendar access, so Resbite shows "We couldn’t open your calendar. Add to calendar needs iOS 17 or later." instead of asking.
- **P11. Reminder times are stored with each reminder** (`data.at`). Expo 57 on iOS reports a date trigger back as a time interval, so without the stored time every sync would cancel and reschedule everything.
- **P12. Signing out also clears reminders already shown** in Notification Center, not only scheduled ones.

### Task 1: End time, private Meeting details and saving (`ux3_event_details`, slice 1)

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`
- Create: `supabase/tests/ux3.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/deletion.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/tests.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (database):
  - `public.plans.ends_at timestamptz null` (after `starts_at`), `public.plans.has_meeting_details boolean`; `public.plans.note` is always `''`.
  - `public.plan_meeting_details(plan_id uuid primary key → plans.id, note text, updated_at)`: readable by the organizer and attendees whose answer is `accepted` or `maybe`; no client writes. PostgREST can embed it from `plans` as `plan_meeting_details(note)`.
  - `public.create_plan_v3(p_id uuid, p_title text, p_description text, p_categories text[], p_activity text, p_start timestamptz, p_ends timestamptz, p_zone text, p_place text, p_note text default '', p_lat double precision default null, p_lon double precision default null) returns uuid`
  - `public.change_plan_v3(p_plan uuid, p_version integer, p_title text, p_description text, p_categories text[], p_start timestamptz, p_ends timestamptz, p_zone text, p_place text, p_note text, p_cancel boolean default false) returns integer`
  - `p_note` is the Meeting details text. The functions raise `22023` with "The end must be after the start" for a bad end.
  - `create_plan_v2` / `change_plan_v2` keep their signatures and delegate (v2 keeps an end's length).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing SQL test** `supabase/tests/ux3.sql`:

<!-- block: task1-ux3-test -->
```sql
-- UX3 event details (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-ux3-design.md). Synthetic identities only.
-- One rolled-back block per slice; each block uses its own UUID prefix family (f1, f2, f3).

-- Block 1: the optional end time and saving (Task 1).
begin;
insert into auth.users(id,email,email_confirmed_at,raw_user_meta_data) values
 ('f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','ux3-1-owner@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002','ux3-1-guest@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}');
insert into private.tester_roster(email) values ('ux3-1-owner@resbite-test.invalid'),('ux3-1-guest@resbite-test.invalid');
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
select public.save_profile('Owner one');
do $$ begin
 -- v3 saves an end after the start; a retry with the same payload is acknowledged, a different end is not.
 perform public.create_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','End fixture','',array['community'],null,
  '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 12:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512');
 perform public.create_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','End fixture','',array['community'],null,
  '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 12:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512');
 begin
  perform public.create_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','End fixture','',array['community'],null,
   '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 13:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512');
  raise exception 'Retry with a different end accepted';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
 begin
  perform public.create_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002','Bad end','',array['community'],null,
   '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 10:00+00','UTC','Park','');
  raise exception 'An end equal to the start was accepted';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
 -- An older build (v2) creates without an end; its note becomes Meeting details.
 perform public.create_plan_v2('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003','Old build','',array['community'],null,
  '2099-06-02 10:00+00','UTC','Park','Old note');
end $$;
select public.create_invite('f1200000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',repeat('a',64));
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002","role":"authenticated"}',true);
select public.save_profile('Gia');
select public.claim_invite(repeat('a',64));
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 -- Changing only the end time saves without a change notice.
 if public.change_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',1,'End fixture','',array['community'],
   '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 13:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512')<>2 then raise exception 'End change not saved as version 2'; end if;
 begin
  perform public.change_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',2,'End fixture','',array['community'],
   '2099-06-01 10:00+00','2099-06-01 09:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512');
  raise exception 'An end before the start was accepted';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
 -- An older build moving the start keeps the length (3 hours).
 if public.change_plan_v2('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',2,'End fixture','',array['community'],
   '2099-06-01 11:00+00','UTC','Park','Gate 4512')<>3 then raise exception 'v2 move not saved'; end if;
 -- v3 can clear the end.
 if public.change_plan_v3('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',3,'End fixture','',array['community'],
   '2099-06-01 11:00+00',null,'UTC','Park','Gate 4512')<>4 then raise exception 'Clearing the end not saved'; end if;
 -- An older build moving a start without an end keeps none.
 if public.change_plan_v2('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003',1,'Old build','',array['community'],
   '2099-06-02 12:00+00','UTC','Park','Old note')<>2 then raise exception 'v2 move without end not saved'; end if;
end $$;
reset role;
do $$ begin
 if (select ends_at from public.plans where id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is not null then raise exception 'End not cleared'; end if;
 if (select ends_at from public.plans where id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003') is not null then raise exception 'v2 invented an end'; end if;
 -- Older builds' edits keep Meeting details (v2 writes its note there).
 if (select note from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is distinct from 'Gate 4512' then raise exception 'v2 edit lost Meeting details'; end if;
 if (select note from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003') is distinct from 'Old note' then raise exception 'v2 create lost Meeting details'; end if;
 -- Notices: none for the end-only change (version 2); one time notice for the v2 move (version 3); none for clearing (version 4).
 if (select string_agg(dedupe_key||'='||detail,',' order by id) from private.notification_outbox
     where plan_id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and kind='plan_changed')
    is distinct from 'plan:f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001:3:f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002=time' then
  raise exception 'Change notices wrong for end-time changes';
 end if;
 -- The constraint itself.
 begin
  update public.plans set ends_at=starts_at where id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
  raise exception 'Stored end equal to start';
 exception when check_violation then null; end;
end $$;
-- The kept length, checked on a fresh copy of the move.
do $$ begin
 if exists(select 1 from public.plans where id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and title<>'End fixture') then raise exception 'Title lost'; end if;
end $$;
rollback;

begin;
-- Block 1b: v2 keeps the length exactly (separate so the stored end is visible before clearing).
insert into auth.users(id,email,email_confirmed_at,raw_user_meta_data) values
 ('f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003','ux3-1b-owner@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}');
insert into private.tester_roster(email) values ('ux3-1b-owner@resbite-test.invalid');
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f1000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003","role":"authenticated"}',true);

select public.change_plan_v2('f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004',1,'Length','',array['community'],

do $$ begin
 if (select ends_at from public.plans where id='f1100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004') is distinct from '2099-06-03 10:30+00'::timestamptz then
  raise exception 'v2 did not keep the 2.5-hour length';

 from generate_series(2,6) n;

 for n in 2..7 loop
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f2000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000'||n||'","role":"authenticated"}',true);
  perform public.save_profile('Guest '||n);

 end loop;
end $$;
reset role;
update public.attendees set response=case user_id
 when 'f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002' then 'accepted' when 'f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003' then 'maybe'
 when 'f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000005' then 'declined' when 'f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000006' then 'withdrawn'
 else 'pending' end where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
do $$ begin
 if (select note from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is distinct from 'Gate 4512' then raise exception 'Meeting details not stored'; end if;
 if (select note from public.plans where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001')<>'' then raise exception 'The note stayed on the event row'; end if;
 if not (select has_meeting_details from public.plans where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Flag not set'; end if;
end $$;
-- Who can read it: the organizer, Going and Maybe; nobody else, while everyone invited still reads the event.
create temporary table ux3_seen(n int, details int, events int) on commit drop;
grant all on ux3_seen to authenticated;
set local role authenticated;
do $$ declare n int; begin
 for n in 1..7 loop
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f2000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000'||n||'","role":"authenticated"}',true);
  insert into ux3_seen values(n,
   (select count(*) from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'),
   (select count(*) from public.plans where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and has_meeting_details));
 end loop;
end $$;
reset role;
do $$ begin
 if (select string_agg(n||':'||details||':'||events,',' order by n) from ux3_seen)
    is distinct from '1:1:1,2:1:1,3:1:1,4:0:1,5:0:1,6:0:1,7:0:0' then
  raise exception 'Meeting details visibility wrong: %', (select string_agg(n||':'||details||':'||events,',' order by n) from ux3_seen);
 end if;
end $$;
-- Changing to Can't go hides them again; nobody can write the table directly.
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002","role":"authenticated"}',true);
select public.respond('f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','declined',(select version from public.attendees where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and user_id='f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002'));
do $$ begin
 if exists(select 1 from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Can''t go still reads Meeting details'; end if;
end $$;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 begin insert into public.plan_meeting_details(plan_id,note) values('f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','x'); raise exception 'Direct insert allowed';
 exception when insufficient_privilege then null; end;
 begin update public.plan_meeting_details set note='x'; raise exception 'Direct update allowed';
 exception when insufficient_privilege then null; end;
 begin delete from public.plan_meeting_details; raise exception 'Direct delete allowed';
 exception when insufficient_privilege then null; end;
 begin perform private.write_meeting_details('f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','x'); raise exception 'Helper callable by testers';
 exception when insufficient_privilege then null; end;
 -- Saving empty Meeting details removes them.
 perform public.change_plan_v3('f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',1,'Private fixture','',array['community'],
  '2099-07-01 10:00+00',null,'UTC','Park','');
 if exists(select 1 from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Empty Meeting details kept'; end if;
 if (select has_meeting_details from public.plans where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Flag kept after removal'; end if;
 perform public.change_plan_v3('f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',2,'Private fixture','',array['community'],
  '2099-07-01 10:00+00',null,'UTC','Park','Back door');
end $$;
reset role;
do $$ begin
 -- The old column can never hold text again.
 begin update public.plans set note='leak' where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'; raise exception 'Note column accepted text';
 exception when check_violation then null; end;
 -- Account deletion's redaction removes Meeting details with the other text.
 update public.profiles set deletion_requested_at=now() where id='f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
 update public.plans set status='cancelled' where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
 if exists(select 1 from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Redaction kept Meeting details'; end if;
 if (select has_meeting_details from public.plans where id='f2100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') then raise exception 'Redaction kept the flag'; end if;
 -- Deleting an event deletes its Meeting details.
 update public.profiles set deletion_requested_at=null where id='f2000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
end $$;
rollback;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration** `supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`:

<!-- block: task1-migration -->
```sql
-- UX3 event details (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-ux3-design.md): an optional end time, private
-- Meeting details for the organizer and Going or Maybe guests, and a pinned Note to guests.
-- Hosted-compatible: depends only on the migrations listed in supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/migrations.txt.
-- Rollback: supabase/rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql.

-- Slice 1: the optional end time. Builds older than UX3 can't set one; when they move the start, the
-- end moves by the same amount, so the length is kept.
alter table public.plans add column ends_at timestamptz,
 add constraint plans_ends_after_start check (ends_at is null or ends_at>starts_at);

-- Slice 2: Meeting details (UX3-D5, UX3-D6). The text leaves the event row, which every invitee can read,
-- for its own table, which only the organizer and guests who answered Going or Maybe can read. Nobody
-- writes it directly; the save functions below do.
-- Every invitee may know that Meeting details exist (so the locked line is honest); only the people above
-- may read them.
alter table public.plans add column has_meeting_details boolean not null default false;
create table public.plan_meeting_details (
 plan_id uuid primary key references public.plans(id) on delete cascade,
 note text not null check (note<>'' and char_length(note)<=2000),
 updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
alter table public.plan_meeting_details enable row level security;
revoke all on public.plan_meeting_details from public, anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.plan_meeting_details to authenticated;

create function private.can_read_meeting_details(p_plan uuid) returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = '' as $$
 select private.eligible() and exists(select 1 from public.plans p where p.id=p_plan and (p.owner_id=auth.uid()
  or exists(select 1 from public.attendees a where a.plan_id=p.id and a.user_id=auth.uid() and a.response in ('accepted','maybe'))))
$$;
revoke all on function private.can_read_meeting_details(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function private.can_read_meeting_details(uuid) to authenticated;
create policy meeting_details_read on public.plan_meeting_details for select to authenticated
 using (private.can_read_meeting_details(plan_id));

-- Removal is its own definer function so the deletion redaction trigger can call it whoever updates the plan.
create function private.forget_meeting_details(p_plan uuid) returns void language sql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
 delete from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id=p_plan
$$;
-- An empty text removes the row; the same text leaves it (and its time) alone. Keeps the flag in step.
create function private.write_meeting_details(p_plan uuid,p_note text) returns void language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
begin
 if coalesce(p_note,'')='' then
  perform private.forget_meeting_details(p_plan);
  update public.plans set has_meeting_details=false where id=p_plan and has_meeting_details;
 else
  insert into public.plan_meeting_details(plan_id,note) values(p_plan,p_note)
  on conflict (plan_id) do update set note=excluded.note,updated_at=now()
  where public.plan_meeting_details.note is distinct from excluded.note;
  update public.plans set has_meeting_details=true where id=p_plan and not has_meeting_details;
 end if;
end $$;
revoke all on function private.forget_meeting_details(uuid), private.write_meeting_details(uuid,text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;

-- Existing notes move over intact; the old column then stays empty for good.
insert into public.plan_meeting_details(plan_id,note) select id,note from public.plans where note<>'';
update public.plans set note='',has_meeting_details=true where note<>'';
alter table public.plans add constraint plans_note_moved check (note='');

-- Account deletion marks the profile before it updates each owned plan; while the mark is present no
-- update leaves organizer-written text behind, now including the Meeting details.
-- Otherwise the 20261014120000_ux2_rules body (create or replace keeps its privileges).
create or replace function private.redact_deleting_owner_plan() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = '' as $$
begin
 if new.owner_id is not null and exists(
  select 1 from public.profiles as p where p.id = new.owner_id and p.deletion_requested_at is not null) then
  new.title := 'Resbite';
  new.description := '';
  new.cover_path := null;
  new.cover_description := '';
  new.has_meeting_details := false;
  perform private.forget_meeting_details(new.id);
 end if;
 return new;
end $$;

-- Saving: v3 takes the end time and writes Meeting details; v2 keeps its signature for builds older than
-- UX3 and delegates, keeping the event's length when the start moves.
create function private.create_plan_v3(p_id uuid,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_activity text,p_start timestamptz,p_ends timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text default '',p_lat double precision default null,p_lon double precision default null)
returns uuid language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_uid uuid := private.require_tester(); v_existing public.plans;
begin
 -- Serialize repeated creation IDs; a retry must match the original payload exactly.
 perform pg_catalog.pg_advisory_xact_lock(pg_catalog.hashtextextended(p_id::text,0));
 select * into v_existing from public.plans where id=p_id;
 if found then
  if v_existing.owner_id<>v_uid or v_existing.title is distinct from btrim(p_title)
  or v_existing.description is distinct from coalesce(p_description,'') or v_existing.categories is distinct from p_categories
  or v_existing.activity_id is distinct from p_activity or v_existing.starts_at is distinct from p_start
  or v_existing.ends_at is distinct from p_ends
  or v_existing.time_zone is distinct from p_zone or v_existing.place_label is distinct from btrim(p_place)
  or coalesce((select note from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id=p_id),'') is distinct from coalesce(p_note,'')
  or v_existing.latitude is distinct from p_lat or v_existing.longitude is distinct from p_lon then
   raise exception 'Creation identifier already used' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  return p_id;
 end if;
 if p_title is null or p_categories is null then raise exception 'Title and categories required' using errcode='23502'; end if;
 if p_start is null or p_start<=now() or not exists(select 1 from pg_catalog.pg_timezone_names where name=p_zone) then raise exception 'Invalid future schedule' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 if p_ends is not null and p_ends<=p_start then raise exception 'The end must be after the start' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 if p_activity is not null and not exists(select 1 from public.activities where id=p_activity and published) then raise exception 'Activity unavailable' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 insert into public.plans(id,owner_id,activity_id,title,description,categories,starts_at,ends_at,time_zone,place_label,latitude,longitude)
 values(p_id,v_uid,p_activity,btrim(p_title),coalesce(p_description,''),p_categories,p_start,p_ends,p_zone,btrim(p_place),p_lat,p_lon);
 perform private.write_meeting_details(p_id,coalesce(p_note,''));
 return p_id;
end $$;

-- Otherwise the 20261014120000_ux2_rules change_plan_v2 body: only a new start time or place notifies (an
-- end-time change doesn't), and only people who may still come; cancelling tells everyone.
create function private.change_plan_v3(p_plan uuid,p_version integer,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_start timestamptz,p_ends timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text,p_cancel boolean default false,p_keep_length boolean default false)
returns integer language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_uid uuid := private.require_tester(); v_plan public.plans; v_next integer; v_cancel boolean := coalesce(p_cancel,false);
 v_time boolean; v_place boolean; v_ends timestamptz;
begin
 select * into v_plan from public.plans where id=p_plan for update;
 if not found or v_plan.owner_id<>v_uid then raise exception 'Owner required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if v_plan.status<>'active' or v_plan.starts_at<=now() then raise exception 'Plan unavailable' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 if p_version is distinct from v_plan.version then raise exception 'Plan changed; refresh' using errcode='40001'; end if;
 if not v_cancel and (p_start is null or p_start<=now() or not exists(select 1 from pg_catalog.pg_timezone_names where name=p_zone)) then raise exception 'Invalid future schedule' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 v_ends := case when p_keep_length then p_start+(v_plan.ends_at-v_plan.starts_at) else p_ends end;
 if not v_cancel and v_ends is not null and v_ends<=p_start then raise exception 'The end must be after the start' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 v_next := v_plan.version+1;
 if v_cancel then
  -- Terminal; the other arguments are ignored.
  update public.plans set status='cancelled',version=v_next where id=p_plan;
  update private.invitations set revoked_at=now() where plan_id=p_plan and revoked_at is null;
  insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,actor_id)
  select p_plan,a.user_id,'plan_cancelled','plan:'||p_plan||':'||v_next||':'||a.user_id,v_uid
  from public.attendees a where a.plan_id=p_plan and a.user_id<>v_uid on conflict do nothing;
 else
  -- A zone change at the same instant moves the shown time, so it counts as a time change.
  v_time := p_start is distinct from v_plan.starts_at or p_zone is distinct from v_plan.time_zone;
  v_place := btrim(p_place) is distinct from v_plan.place_label;
  update public.plans set title=btrim(p_title),description=coalesce(p_description,''),categories=p_categories,
   starts_at=p_start,ends_at=v_ends,time_zone=p_zone,place_label=btrim(p_place),latitude=null,longitude=null,version=v_next
  where id=p_plan;
  perform private.write_meeting_details(p_plan,coalesce(p_note,''));
  if v_time or v_place then
   insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,actor_id,detail)
   select p_plan,a.user_id,'plan_changed','plan:'||p_plan||':'||v_next||':'||a.user_id,v_uid,
    case when v_time and v_place then 'time_place' when v_time then 'time' else 'place' end
   from public.attendees a
   where a.plan_id=p_plan and a.user_id<>v_uid and a.response in ('accepted','maybe','pending')
   on conflict do nothing;
  end if;
 end if;
 return v_next;
end $$;

create or replace function private.create_plan_v2(p_id uuid,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_activity text,p_start timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text default '',p_lat double precision default null,p_lon double precision default null)
returns uuid language sql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
 select private.create_plan_v3(p_id,p_title,p_description,p_categories,p_activity,p_start,null,p_zone,p_place,p_note,p_lat,p_lon)
$$;
create or replace function private.change_plan_v2(p_plan uuid,p_version integer,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_start timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text,p_cancel boolean default false)
returns integer language sql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
 select private.change_plan_v3(p_plan,p_version,p_title,p_description,p_categories,p_start,null,p_zone,p_place,p_note,p_cancel,true)
$$;

create function public.create_plan_v3(p_id uuid,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_activity text,p_start timestamptz,p_ends timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text default '',p_lat double precision default null,p_lon double precision default null)
returns uuid language sql security invoker set search_path='' as $$
 select private.create_plan_v3(p_id,p_title,p_description,p_categories,p_activity,p_start,p_ends,p_zone,p_place,p_note,p_lat,p_lon)
$$;
create function public.change_plan_v3(p_plan uuid,p_version integer,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_start timestamptz,p_ends timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text,p_cancel boolean default false)
returns integer language sql security invoker set search_path='' as $$
 select private.change_plan_v3(p_plan,p_version,p_title,p_description,p_categories,p_start,p_ends,p_zone,p_place,p_note,p_cancel,false)
$$;
revoke all on function
 private.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision),
 public.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision),
 private.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean,boolean),
 public.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)
 from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function
 private.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision),
 public.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision),
 private.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean,boolean),
 public.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)
 to authenticated;
```

### Task 2: Note to guests, its delivery check and the "can’t go" wording (`ux3_event_details`, slice 2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261015130000_ux3_note_delivery.sql` (local-only)
- Modify: `supabase/tests/ux3.sql`, `supabase/tests/deletion.sql`, `supabase/tests/notifications.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/functions/_shared/notification-copy.ts`, `supabase/functions/_shared/notification-copy.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's tables and functions.
- Produces:
  - `public.plans.guest_note text` (`''` when none, at most 280 characters, trimmed) and `public.plans.guest_note_at timestamptz null` (set exactly when there is a note).
  - `public.set_guest_note(p_plan uuid, p_note text) returns jsonb` (`{"guest_note": text, "guest_note_at": timestamptz|null}`), for the organizer only.
    - It works until the event ends (`ends_at`, or `starts_at` plus 1 hour) and raises `22023` after that, on cancelled events or over 280 characters, and `42501` for anyone else.
    - It never changes `plans.version`. It records one `guest_note` notice per guest whose answer is `accepted`, `maybe` or `pending`.
  - `notification_outbox.kind` allows `guest_note`.
  - The worker's `notificationCopy` handles `guest_note` ("Sam posted a note to guests.") and says "can’t go" / "can’t go now".

```sql
-- Block 3: Note to guests (Task 2).
begin;
insert into auth.users(id,email,email_confirmed_at,raw_user_meta_data) values
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','ux3-3-owner@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002','ux3-3-going@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003','ux3-3-maybe@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004','ux3-3-pending@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000005','ux3-3-declined@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}'),
 ('f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000006','ux3-3-withdrawn@resbite-test.invalid',now(),'{}');
insert into private.tester_roster(email) select email from auth.users where email like 'ux3-3-%@resbite-test.invalid';
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
select public.save_profile('Owner three');
select public.create_plan_v3('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Note fixture','',array['community'],null,
 '2099-08-01 10:00+00',null,'UTC','Park','');
select public.create_invite(('f3200000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000'||n)::uuid,'f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',repeat(chr(96+n),64))
 from generate_series(2,6) n;
do $$ declare n int; begin
 for n in 2..6 loop
  perform set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000'||n||'","role":"authenticated"}',true);
  perform public.save_profile('Guest '||n);
  perform public.claim_invite(repeat(chr(96+n),64));
 end loop;
end $$;
reset role;
update public.attendees set response=case user_id
 when 'f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002' then 'accepted' when 'f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003' then 'maybe'
 when 'f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000005' then 'declined' when 'f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000006' then 'withdrawn'
 else 'pending' end where plan_id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
delete from private.notification_outbox where plan_id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ declare r jsonb; begin
 r := public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','  Bring a blanket  ');
 if r->>'guest_note' is distinct from 'Bring a blanket' or r->>'guest_note_at' is null then raise exception 'Note not posted: %', r; end if;
 -- The same text again is acknowledged without a second notice.
 perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Bring a blanket');
 begin perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',repeat('x',281)); raise exception 'Long note accepted';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
end $$;
-- Guests read the note; a guest can't post one.
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 if (select guest_note from public.plans where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is distinct from 'Bring a blanket' then raise exception 'Unanswered guest cannot read the note'; end if;
 begin perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Hijack'); raise exception 'Guest posted a note';
 exception when insufficient_privilege then null; end;
end $$;
reset role;
do $$ begin
 -- One notice each for Going, Maybe and unanswered guests, naming the organizer; none for Can't go or the organizer.
 if (select string_agg(recipient_id::text,',' order by recipient_id) from private.notification_outbox
     where plan_id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and kind='guest_note'
       and actor_id='f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and detail is null)
    is distinct from 'f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002,f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003,f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004' then
  raise exception 'Note notices went to the wrong people';
 end if;
 -- The note never changes the event version.
 if (select version from public.plans where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001')<>1 then raise exception 'Note changed the version'; end if;
 -- A note notice carries no detail.
 begin
  insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,detail)
  values('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002','guest_note','ux3-bad-1','time');
  raise exception 'Note notice accepted a detail';
 exception when check_violation then null; end;
end $$;
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ declare r jsonb; begin
 -- Editing records a second notice; removing records none and clears the time.
 perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Running 10 minutes late');
 r := public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','');
 if r->>'guest_note'<>'' or r->>'guest_note_at' is not null then raise exception 'Note not removed: %', r; end if;
end $$;
reset role;
do $$ begin
 if (select count(*) from private.notification_outbox where plan_id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and kind='guest_note')<>6 then
  raise exception 'Expected 3 notices for the post and 3 for the edit, none for the removal';
 end if;
 -- Started events take notes until the end (or an hour after the start); not after, and never when cancelled.
 update public.plans set starts_at=now()-interval '30 minutes' where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
end $$;
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Running late');
end $$;
reset role;
update public.plans set starts_at=now()-interval '2 hours' where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 begin perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Too late'); raise exception 'Note accepted after the end';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
end $$;
reset role;
update public.plans set ends_at=now()+interval '1 hour' where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Still here until the end');
end $$;
reset role;
update public.plans set status='cancelled' where id='f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
set local role authenticated;
select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{"sub":"f3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001","role":"authenticated"}',true);
do $$ begin
 begin perform public.set_guest_note('f3100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','After cancelling'); raise exception 'Note accepted on a cancelled event';
 exception when invalid_parameter_value then null; end;
end $$;
reset role;
rollback;
```

  2. Append at the end of the file:

<!-- block: task2-migration-tail -->
```sql

-- Slice 3: Note to guests (UX3-D5). One short announcement with the time it was posted.
alter table public.plans add column guest_note text not null default '',
 add column guest_note_at timestamptz,
 add constraint plans_guest_note_valid check (
  guest_note=btrim(guest_note) and char_length(guest_note)<=280 and ((guest_note='')=(guest_note_at is null)));

-- A posted note is recorded for a notification; nothing is sent while sending stays off. Replaced by
-- exact name: if the live name differs, this fails instead of leaving two checks.
alter table private.notification_outbox drop constraint notification_outbox_kind_check;
alter table private.notification_outbox add constraint notification_outbox_kind_check
 check (kind in ('invitation','rsvp','plan_changed','plan_cancelled','guest_note'));

-- The organizer posts, edits or removes the note until the event ends (its end time, or an hour after the
-- start). It never changes the event's version, so it can't conflict with an edit. Posting or changing it
-- records one notice for each guest who may come; removing it records nothing.
create function private.set_guest_note(p_plan uuid,p_note text) returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_uid uuid := private.require_tester(); v_plan public.plans; v_text text := btrim(coalesce(p_note,'')); v_at timestamptz;
begin
 if char_length(v_text)>280 then raise exception 'Note too long' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 select * into v_plan from public.plans where id=p_plan for update;
 if not found or v_plan.owner_id<>v_uid then raise exception 'Owner required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if v_plan.status<>'active' or now()>=coalesce(v_plan.ends_at,v_plan.starts_at+interval '1 hour') then
  raise exception 'Plan unavailable' using errcode='22023';
 end if;
 if v_text=v_plan.guest_note then
  return jsonb_build_object('guest_note',v_plan.guest_note,'guest_note_at',v_plan.guest_note_at);
 end if;
 v_at := case when v_text='' then null else pg_catalog.clock_timestamp() end;
 update public.plans set guest_note=v_text,guest_note_at=v_at where id=p_plan;
 if v_text<>'' then
  insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,actor_id)
  select p_plan,a.user_id,'guest_note','note:'||p_plan||':'||pg_catalog.to_char(v_at,'YYYYMMDDHH24MISSUS')||':'||a.user_id,v_uid
  from public.attendees a where a.plan_id=p_plan and a.user_id<>v_uid and a.response in ('accepted','maybe','pending')
  on conflict do nothing;
 end if;
 return jsonb_build_object('guest_note',v_text,'guest_note_at',v_at);
end $$;
create function public.set_guest_note(p_plan uuid,p_note text) returns jsonb language sql security invoker set search_path = '' as $$
 select private.set_guest_note(p_plan,p_note)
$$;
revoke all on function private.set_guest_note(uuid,text), public.set_guest_note(uuid,text) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function private.set_guest_note(uuid,text), public.set_guest_note(uuid,text) to authenticated;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the local-only delivery check** `supabase/migrations/20261015130000_ux3_note_delivery.sql`:

<!-- block: task2-note-delivery -->
```sql
-- UX3 Note to guests delivery check. LOCAL-ONLY: replaces a function from
-- 20261014130000_ux2_notification_wording.sql, which the hosted project does not run.
-- It goes live with the notification sender, after that has its own owner approval, never with the UX3 release.

-- A change notice or a Note to guests goes only to people who may still come, checked again at send time.
-- Otherwise the 20261014130000_ux2_notification_wording body.
create or replace function private.notification_recipient_eligible(p_outbox bigint) returns boolean
language sql stable security definer set search_path='' as $$
 select exists (
 select 1 from private.notification_outbox o
 join public.profiles p on p.id=o.recipient_id
 join auth.users u on u.id=p.id
 join private.tester_roster r on r.email=lower(u.email) and r.enabled
 join public.plans plan on plan.id=o.plan_id
 where o.id=p_outbox and p.deletion_requested_at is null
 and u.email_confirmed_at is not null and not coalesce(u.is_anonymous,false)
 and (u.banned_until is null or u.banned_until<=now())
 and (plan.owner_id=p.id or exists(select 1 from public.attendees a where a.plan_id=plan.id and a.user_id=p.id
  and (o.kind not in ('plan_changed','guest_note') or a.response in ('accepted','maybe','pending'))))
 and o.created_at>now()-interval '24 hours'
 )
$$;
```

### Task 3: Rollback script and hosted rehearsal

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql`
- Create: `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.before.sql`
- Create: `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.after.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the full migration from Tasks 1–2.
- Produces: a rollback that restores the exact pre-UX3 `create_plan_v2`, `change_plan_v2` and `redact_deleting_owner_plan` definitions, the constraints, the grants and every note. The hosted rehearsal proves it, then undoes it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the rehearsal (it fails until the rollback exists).** Create `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.before.sql`:

<!-- block: task3-before -->
```sql
-- Rehearsal for 20261015120000_ux3_event_details (hosted-baseline mode only); .after.sql removes everything here.
-- 1. Data in the pre-UX3 shape: an event with a note, one without, and a queued notice.
insert into auth.users(id,email,email_confirmed_at) values
 ('f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','ux3-rehearsal-owner@resbite-test.invalid',now());
insert into public.profiles(id,display_name) values ('f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Rehearsal owner');
insert into public.plans(id,owner_id,title,description,categories,starts_at,time_zone,place_label,note) values
 ('f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','With note','',array['community'],now()+interval '1 day','UTC','Park','Gate 4512'),
 ('f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002','f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','Without note','',array['community'],now()+interval '2 days','UTC','Park','');
insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key)
 values('f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','rsvp','ux3-rehearsal-1');

-- 2. The exact definitions UX3 replaces, so .after.sql can prove the rollback restores them.
create function private.ux3_rehearsal_snapshot() returns table(name text, definition text)
language sql stable set search_path='' as $$
 select p.oid::regprocedure::text, pg_catalog.pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) from pg_catalog.pg_proc p
 where p.oid in ('private.create_plan_v2(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision)'::regprocedure,
  'private.change_plan_v2(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)'::regprocedure,
  'private.redact_deleting_owner_plan()'::regprocedure)
 union all
 select 'constraint '||conname, pg_catalog.pg_get_constraintdef(oid) from pg_catalog.pg_constraint
 where conrelid in ('public.plans'::regclass,'private.notification_outbox'::regclass)
 union all
 select 'columns '||c.relname, string_agg(a.attname||':'||pg_catalog.format_type(a.atttypid,a.atttypmod), ',' order by a.attnum)
 from pg_catalog.pg_attribute a join pg_catalog.pg_class c on c.oid=a.attrelid
 where c.oid='public.plans'::regclass and a.attnum>0 and not a.attisdropped group by c.relname
 union all
 select 'grants', string_agg(f||':'||r||':'||pg_catalog.has_function_privilege(r,f,'execute')::text, ',' order by f, r)
 from unnest(array['public.create_plan_v2(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision)',
  'private.create_plan_v2(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision)',
  'public.change_plan_v2(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)',
  'private.change_plan_v2(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)']) f,
  unnest(array['anon','authenticated','service_role']) r
$$;
revoke all on function private.ux3_rehearsal_snapshot() from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
create table private.ux3_rehearsal_baseline as select * from private.ux3_rehearsal_snapshot();
alter table private.ux3_rehearsal_baseline enable row level security;
```

and `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.after.sql`:

<!-- block: task3-after -->
```sql
-- Rehearsal checks for 20261015120000_ux3_event_details (hosted-baseline mode only).
-- 1. Pre-UX3 data: the note moved intact to Meeting details, the empty one made no row, the notice is untouched.
do $$ begin
 if (select note from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is distinct from 'Gate 4512' then raise exception 'Note not moved'; end if;
 if (select string_agg(has_meeting_details::text,',' order by id) from public.plans where id::text like 'f91%')<>'true,false' then raise exception 'Flags wrong after the move'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from public.plan_meeting_details where plan_id='f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002') then raise exception 'Empty note made a row'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from public.plans where note<>'') then raise exception 'A note stayed on an event'; end if;
 if exists(select 1 from public.plans where id::text like 'f91%' and (ends_at is not null or guest_note<>'' or guest_note_at is not null)) then raise exception 'New columns not empty'; end if;
 if not exists(select 1 from private.notification_outbox where dedupe_key='ux3-rehearsal-1') then raise exception 'Queued notice lost'; end if;
end $$;
-- 2. The rollback restores every replaced definition, the notes and the grants exactly; rehearsed, then undone.
begin;
update public.plans set ends_at=starts_at+interval '1 hour',guest_note='Hello',guest_note_at=now() where id='f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
\ir ../../rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql
do $$ declare item record; begin
 for item in
  select coalesce(b.name,n.name) as name, b.definition as before, n.definition as after
  from private.ux3_rehearsal_baseline b full join private.ux3_rehearsal_snapshot() n on n.name=b.name
 loop
  if item.before is distinct from item.after then raise exception 'Rollback did not restore %', item.name; end if;
 end loop;
 if (select note from public.plans where id='f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001') is distinct from 'Gate 4512' then raise exception 'Rollback lost the note'; end if;
 if to_regclass('public.plan_meeting_details') is not null
    or to_regprocedure('public.set_guest_note(uuid,text)') is not null
    or to_regprocedure('private.can_read_meeting_details(uuid)') is not null then
  raise exception 'Rollback left UX3 objects';
 end if;
end $$;
rollback;
-- 3. Remove the seed and the rehearsal helpers so later tests start clean.
delete from private.notification_outbox where plan_id in ('f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
delete from public.plans where id in ('f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001','f9100000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002');
delete from public.profiles where id='f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
delete from auth.users where id='f9000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';
drop table private.ux3_rehearsal_baseline;
drop function private.ux3_rehearsal_snapshot();
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the rollback** `supabase/rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql`. The three function bodies are copied verbatim from `20261007150000_custom_events.sql` (`create_plan_v2`, with `create function` changed to `create or replace function`) and `20261014120000_ux2_rules.sql` (`change_plan_v2`, `redact_deleting_owner_plan`). The rehearsal fails on any difference.

<!-- block: task3-rollback -->
```sql
-- Rollback for 20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql. Run as one transaction, only with the owner's approval:
--   begin; \i supabase/rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql; commit;
-- It puts Meeting details back in plans.note, restores the replaced functions verbatim, and removes
-- the end time, the Note to guests and their notices.

alter table public.plans drop constraint plans_note_moved;
update public.plans as p set note=m.note from public.plan_meeting_details as m where m.plan_id=p.id;

-- The 20261007150000_custom_events body, verbatim.
create or replace function private.create_plan_v2(p_id uuid,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_activity text,p_start timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text default '',p_lat double precision default null,p_lon double precision default null)
returns uuid language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_uid uuid := private.require_tester(); v_existing public.plans;
begin
 -- Serialize repeated creation IDs; a retry must match the original payload exactly.
 perform pg_catalog.pg_advisory_xact_lock(pg_catalog.hashtextextended(p_id::text,0));
 select * into v_existing from public.plans where id=p_id;
 if found then
  if v_existing.owner_id<>v_uid or v_existing.title is distinct from btrim(p_title)
  or v_existing.description is distinct from coalesce(p_description,'') or v_existing.categories is distinct from p_categories
  or v_existing.activity_id is distinct from p_activity or v_existing.starts_at is distinct from p_start
  or v_existing.time_zone is distinct from p_zone or v_existing.place_label is distinct from btrim(p_place)
  or v_existing.note is distinct from coalesce(p_note,'') or v_existing.latitude is distinct from p_lat
  or v_existing.longitude is distinct from p_lon then
   raise exception 'Creation identifier already used' using errcode='22023';
  end if;
  return p_id;
 end if;
 if p_title is null or p_categories is null then raise exception 'Title and categories required' using errcode='23502'; end if;
 if p_start is null or p_start<=now() or not exists(select 1 from pg_catalog.pg_timezone_names where name=p_zone) then raise exception 'Invalid future schedule' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 if p_activity is not null and not exists(select 1 from public.activities where id=p_activity and published) then raise exception 'Activity unavailable' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 insert into public.plans(id,owner_id,activity_id,title,description,categories,starts_at,time_zone,place_label,note,latitude,longitude)
 values(p_id,v_uid,p_activity,btrim(p_title),coalesce(p_description,''),p_categories,p_start,p_zone,btrim(p_place),coalesce(p_note,''),p_lat,p_lon);
 return p_id;
end $$;

-- The 20261014120000_ux2_rules bodies, verbatim.
create or replace function private.change_plan_v2(p_plan uuid,p_version integer,p_title text,p_description text,p_categories text[],p_start timestamptz,p_zone text,p_place text,p_note text,p_cancel boolean default false)
returns integer language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$
declare v_uid uuid := private.require_tester(); v_plan public.plans; v_next integer; v_cancel boolean := coalesce(p_cancel,false);
 v_time boolean; v_place boolean;
begin
 select * into v_plan from public.plans where id=p_plan for update;
 if not found or v_plan.owner_id<>v_uid then raise exception 'Owner required' using errcode='42501'; end if;
 if v_plan.status<>'active' or v_plan.starts_at<=now() then raise exception 'Plan unavailable' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 if p_version is distinct from v_plan.version then raise exception 'Plan changed; refresh' using errcode='40001'; end if;
 if not v_cancel and (p_start is null or p_start<=now() or not exists(select 1 from pg_catalog.pg_timezone_names where name=p_zone)) then raise exception 'Invalid future schedule' using errcode='22023'; end if;
 v_next := v_plan.version+1;
 if v_cancel then
  -- Terminal; the other arguments are ignored.
  update public.plans set status='cancelled',version=v_next where id=p_plan;
  update private.invitations set revoked_at=now() where plan_id=p_plan and revoked_at is null;
  insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,actor_id)
  select p_plan,a.user_id,'plan_cancelled','plan:'||p_plan||':'||v_next||':'||a.user_id,v_uid
  from public.attendees a where a.plan_id=p_plan and a.user_id<>v_uid on conflict do nothing;
 else
  -- A zone change at the same instant moves the shown time, so it counts as a time change.
  v_time := p_start is distinct from v_plan.starts_at or p_zone is distinct from v_plan.time_zone;
  v_place := btrim(p_place) is distinct from v_plan.place_label;
  update public.plans set title=btrim(p_title),description=coalesce(p_description,''),categories=p_categories,
   starts_at=p_start,time_zone=p_zone,place_label=btrim(p_place),note=coalesce(p_note,''),latitude=null,longitude=null,version=v_next
  where id=p_plan;
  if v_time or v_place then
   insert into private.notification_outbox(plan_id,recipient_id,kind,dedupe_key,actor_id,detail)
   select p_plan,a.user_id,'plan_changed','plan:'||p_plan||':'||v_next||':'||a.user_id,v_uid,
    case when v_time and v_place then 'time_place' when v_time then 'time' else 'place' end
   from public.attendees a
   where a.plan_id=p_plan and a.user_id<>v_uid and a.response in ('accepted','maybe','pending')
   on conflict do nothing;
  end if;
 end if;
 return v_next;
end $$;

create or replace function private.redact_deleting_owner_plan() returns trigger
language plpgsql set search_path = '' as $$
begin
 if new.owner_id is not null and exists(
  select 1 from public.profiles as p where p.id = new.owner_id and p.deletion_requested_at is not null) then
  new.title := 'Resbite';
  new.description := '';
  new.cover_path := null;
  new.cover_description := '';
 end if;
 return new;
end $$;

drop function public.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision);
drop function private.create_plan_v3(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision);
drop function public.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean);
drop function private.change_plan_v3(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean,boolean);
drop function public.set_guest_note(uuid,text);
drop function private.set_guest_note(uuid,text);
drop policy meeting_details_read on public.plan_meeting_details;
drop table public.plan_meeting_details;
drop function private.write_meeting_details(uuid,text);
drop function private.forget_meeting_details(uuid);
drop function private.can_read_meeting_details(uuid);

delete from private.notification_outbox where kind='guest_note';
alter table private.notification_outbox drop constraint notification_outbox_kind_check;
alter table private.notification_outbox add constraint notification_outbox_kind_check
 check (kind in ('invitation','rsvp','plan_changed','plan_cancelled'));

alter table public.plans drop constraint plans_guest_note_valid, drop column guest_note, drop column guest_note_at, drop column has_meeting_details;
alter table public.plans drop constraint plans_ends_after_start, drop column ends_at;
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { defaultEnd, shiftedEnd, spokenRange, timeRange, validateEnd } from "./eventTime.ts";

// Local times, built in the test machine's zone like the app's editor values.
const at = (text: string) => new Date(text.replace(" ", "T")).toISOString();

test("time range: start only, same day, next day, and nonsense ends ignored", () => {
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00")), "10:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), null), "10:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), at("2099-10-16 12:00")), "10:00 – 12:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 22:00"), at("2099-10-17 02:00")), "22:00 – Sat 17 02:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), at("2099-10-16 09:00")), "10:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), "garbage"), "10:00");
  assert.equal(timeRange(null, at("2099-10-16 12:00")), "");
});

test("spoken range for VoiceOver", () => {
  const now = new Date(2099, 9, 1);
  assert.equal(spokenRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), at("2099-10-16 12:00"), now), "from 10:00 to 12:00");
  assert.equal(spokenRange(at("2099-10-16 22:00"), at("2099-10-17 02:00"), now), "from 22:00 to 02:00 on Saturday 17 October");
  assert.equal(spokenRange(at("2099-10-16 10:00"), null, now), "");
});

test("end validation: optional, valid, after the start", () => {
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", ""), null);
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-16 12:00"), null);
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 22:00", "2099-10-17 02:00"), null);
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-16 10:00"), "The end must be after the start.");
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-16 09:59"), "The end must be after the start.");
  assert.equal(validateEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-02-30 12:00"), "Choose a valid end time.");
});

test("the end picker starts an hour after the start, and a moved start keeps the length", () => {
  assert.equal(defaultEnd("2099-10-16 10:00"), "2099-10-16 11:00");
  assert.equal(defaultEnd("2099-10-16 23:30"), "2099-10-17 00:30");
  assert.equal(defaultEnd(""), "");
  assert.equal(shiftedEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-18 09:00", "2099-10-16 12:30"), "2099-10-18 11:30");
  assert.equal(shiftedEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-18 09:00", ""), "");
  // An end that wasn't after the old start isn't a length to keep.
  assert.equal(shiftedEnd("2099-10-16 10:00", "2099-10-18 09:00", "2099-10-16 09:00"), "2099-10-16 09:00");
});
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { canPostGuestNote, guestNoteError, guestNoteTime, meetingDetailsView } from "./eventDetails.ts";

test("Meeting details: organizer, Going and Maybe see the text; others see a lock only when some exist", () => {
  const text = "Gate 4512";
  assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: true, response: null, hasDetails: true, text }), { kind: "shown", text });
  assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: false, response: "accepted", hasDetails: true, text }), { kind: "shown", text });
  assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: false, response: "maybe", hasDetails: true, text: "  Gate 4512 " }), { kind: "shown", text });
  for (const response of [null, "pending", "declined", "withdrawn"]) {
    assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: false, response, hasDetails: true, text: "" }), { kind: "locked" }, String(response));
    assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: false, response, hasDetails: false, text: "" }), { kind: "none" }, String(response));
  }
  // An insider with nothing written sees nothing, never an empty card.
  assert.deepEqual(meetingDetailsView({ owner: true, response: null, hasDetails: false, text: "  " }), { kind: "none" });
});

test("Note to guests: 280 characters, posted time, and the posting window", () => {
  assert.equal(guestNoteError("x".repeat(280)), null);
  assert.equal(guestNoteError(` ${"x".repeat(280)} `), null);
  assert.equal(guestNoteError("x".repeat(281)), "Keep the note to 280 characters.");
  const now = new Date(2099, 9, 16, 12, 0);
  assert.equal(guestNoteTime(new Date(2099, 9, 16, 9, 41).toISOString(), now), "9:41");
  assert.equal(guestNoteTime(new Date(2099, 9, 15, 18, 5).toISOString(), now), "Thu 15 18:05");
  assert.equal(guestNoteTime(null, now), "");
  const start = new Date(2099, 9, 16, 10, 0).toISOString();
  const t = (h: number, m = 0) => new Date(2099, 9, 16, h, m).getTime();
  assert.equal(canPostGuestNote({ status: "active", starts_at: start }, t(10, 59)), true);
  assert.equal(canPostGuestNote({ status: "active", starts_at: start }, t(11, 0)), false);
  assert.equal(canPostGuestNote({ status: "active", starts_at: start, ends_at: new Date(2099, 9, 16, 13, 0).toISOString() }, t(12, 30)), true);
  assert.equal(canPostGuestNote({ status: "cancelled", starts_at: start }, t(9)), false);
});
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {
  CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE,
  CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE,
  calendarEventDetails,
  calendarRecordOf,
  canAddToCalendar,
  needsCalendarUpdate,
} from "./calendarEvent.ts";

const plan = {
  title: " Picnic in the park ",
  starts_at: "2099-10-16T09:00:00.000Z",
  ends_at: null as string | null,
  place_label: " Riverside park ",
};

test("the calendar form gets the name, times and place, an hour without an end, no alert and no private text", () => {
  const details = calendarEventDetails(plan);
  assert.deepEqual(details, {
    title: "Picnic in the park",
    startDate: new Date("2099-10-16T09:00:00.000Z"),
    endDate: new Date("2099-10-16T10:00:00.000Z"),
    location: "Riverside park",
    notes: CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE,
    alarms: [],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(calendarEventDetails({ ...plan, ends_at: "2099-10-16T11:30:00.000Z" }).endDate, new Date("2099-10-16T11:30:00.000Z"));
  assert.equal(calendarEventDetails(plan, true).notes, CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE);
  // Even if a plan object carried Meeting details or a link, they never reach the form.
  const leaky = { ...plan, note: "Gate 4512", url: "https://go.resbite.com/invite#token=x" } as typeof plan;
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(calendarEventDetails(leaky)).includes("4512"));
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(calendarEventDetails(leaky)).includes("token"));
});

test("Update calendar shows only after a new start, end or place on an event added here", () => {
  const record = calendarRecordOf(plan);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(undefined, plan), false);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, plan), false);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, { ...plan, starts_at: "2099-10-16T09:00:00Z" }), false);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, { ...plan, title: "Renamed" }), false);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, { ...plan, starts_at: "2099-10-16T10:00:00.000Z" }), true);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, { ...plan, ends_at: "2099-10-16T11:00:00.000Z" }), true);
  assert.equal(needsCalendarUpdate(record, { ...plan, place_label: "Old mill" }), true);
});

test("who sees Add to calendar", () => {
  const active = { status: "active", starts_at: "2099-10-16T09:00:00Z" };
  const now = Date.parse("2099-10-01T00:00:00Z");
  assert.equal(canAddToCalendar(active, { owner: true, response: null }, now), true);
  assert.equal(canAddToCalendar(active, { owner: false, response: "accepted" }, now), true);
  assert.equal(canAddToCalendar(active, { owner: false, response: "maybe" }, now), true);
  for (const response of [null, "pending", "declined", "withdrawn"])
    assert.equal(canAddToCalendar(active, { owner: false, response }, now), false, String(response));
  assert.equal(canAddToCalendar({ ...active, status: "cancelled" }, { owner: true, response: null }, now), false);
  assert.equal(canAddToCalendar(active, { owner: true, response: null }, Date.parse("2099-10-16T09:00:00Z")), false);
});
```

`mobile/src/domain/reminders.test.ts`:

<!-- block: task4-reminders-test -->
```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { REMINDER_LIMIT, REMINDER_PREFIX, plannedReminders, reminderChanges, type ReminderSource } from "./reminders.ts";

const now = new Date(2099, 9, 14, 12, 0);
const event = (over: Partial<ReminderSource> = {}): ReminderSource => ({
  id: "p1",
  title: "Picnic in the park",
  starts_at: new Date(2099, 9, 16, 10, 0).toISOString(),
  status: "active",
  owner: false,
  response: "accepted",
  ...over,
});
const options = { now, enabled: true, muted: new Set<string>() };

test("two reminders, 1 day and 1 hour before, with the name and time only", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(plannedReminders([event()], options), [
    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1d`, planId: "p1", at: new Date(2099, 9, 15, 10, 0), body: "Tomorrow at 10:00 · Picnic in the park" },
    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`, planId: "p1", at: new Date(2099, 9, 16, 9, 0), body: "In 1 hour · Picnic in the park" },
  ]);
});

test("who gets them: the organizer, Going and Maybe; not Can't go, unanswered, cancelled, muted or switched off", () => {
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event({ owner: true, response: null })], options).length, 2);
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event({ response: "maybe" })], options).length, 2);
  for (const response of ["pending", "declined", "withdrawn", null])
    assert.equal(plannedReminders([event({ response })], options).length, 0, String(response));
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event({ status: "cancelled" })], options).length, 0);
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event()], { ...options, muted: new Set(["p1"]) }).length, 0);
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event()], { ...options, enabled: false }).length, 0);
});

test("reminders whose time has passed are skipped; started events have none", () => {
  const soon = event({ starts_at: new Date(2099, 9, 14, 20, 0).toISOString() });
  assert.deepEqual(plannedReminders([soon], options).map((r) => r.id), [`${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`]);
  const imminent = event({ starts_at: new Date(2099, 9, 14, 12, 30).toISOString() });
  assert.deepEqual(plannedReminders([imminent], options), []);
  const started = event({ starts_at: new Date(2099, 9, 14, 11, 0).toISOString() });
  assert.deepEqual(plannedReminders([started], options), []);
});

test("a blank name still reads well, and the phone's limit keeps the soonest", () => {
  assert.equal(plannedReminders([event({ title: "  " })], options)[1].body, "In 1 hour · your resbite");
  const many = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) =>
    event({ id: `p${i}`, starts_at: new Date(2099, 9, 20 + i, 10, 0).toISOString() }),
  );
  const planned = plannedReminders(many, options);
  assert.equal(planned.length, REMINDER_LIMIT);
  assert.equal(planned[0].id, `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p0:1d`);
  assert.ok(planned.every((r, i) => i === 0 || planned[i - 1].at <= r.at));
});

test("syncing moves changed reminders, removes stale ones and never touches other notifications", () => {
  const wanted = plannedReminders([event()], options);
  const [day, hour] = wanted;
  const scheduled = [
    { id: day.id, at: day.at.getTime(), body: day.body },
    { id: hour.id, at: hour.at.getTime() - 60_000, body: hour.body },
    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}gone:1h`, at: 1, body: "In 1 hour · Old" },
    { id: "someone-else", at: 1, body: "Not ours" },
  ];
  const changes = reminderChanges(scheduled, wanted);
  assert.deepEqual(changes.cancel, [hour.id, `${REMINDER_PREFIX}gone:1h`]);
  assert.deepEqual(changes.schedule, [hour]);
  assert.deepEqual(reminderChanges([], []), { cancel: [], schedule: [] });
  // Signing out wants nothing: every Resbite reminder goes.
  assert.deepEqual(reminderChanges(scheduled, []).cancel, [day.id, hour.id, `${REMINDER_PREFIX}gone:1h`]);
});
```
