`mobile/src/domain/eventTime.ts`:

<!-- block: task4-eventTime -->
```ts
import { localDateTime, parseLocalDateTime } from "./plans";
import { localDay, shortDay, spokenDate } from "./schedule";

// UX3 optional end time (spec "Event details"). Editor values stay in the local `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm` format.
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
const clock = (date: Date) => `${pad(date.getHours())}:${pad(date.getMinutes())}`;
const parse = (iso: string | null | undefined) => {
  if (!iso) return null;
  const date = new Date(iso);
  return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) ? date : null;
};

/** Without an end, a calendar entry lasts this long (spec: "a calendar entry lasts 1 hour"). */
export const DEFAULT_LENGTH_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;

/** "10:00 – 12:00"; "22:00 – Sun 11 02:00" when the end is on another day; "10:00" without an end; "" without a start. */
export function timeRange(startIso: string | null, endIso?: string | null): string {
  const start = parse(startIso);
  if (!start) return "";
  const end = parse(endIso);
  if (!end || end <= start) return clock(start);
  const endText = localDay(end) === localDay(start) ? clock(end) : `${shortDay(end)} ${clock(end)}`;
  return `${clock(start)} – ${endText}`;
}

/** How VoiceOver reads the range: "from 10:00 to 12:00", "from 22:00 to 02:00 on Sunday 11 October", or "". */
export function spokenRange(startIso: string | null, endIso: string | null | undefined, now: Date): string {
  const start = parse(startIso);
  const end = parse(endIso);
  if (!start || !end || end <= start) return "";
  const day = localDay(end) === localDay(start) ? "" : ` on ${spokenDate(end, now)}`;
  return `from ${clock(start)} to ${clock(end)}${day}`;
}

/** The editor's end check: none is fine; otherwise a valid local time after the start. */
export function validateEnd(start: string, end: string): string | null {
  if (!end) return null;
  const endDate = parseLocalDateTime(end);
  if (!endDate) return "Choose a valid end time.";
  const startDate = parseLocalDateTime(start);
  if (startDate && endDate.getTime() <= startDate.getTime()) return "The end must be after the start.";
  return null;
}

/** Where "+ End time" starts: an hour after a valid start, else "". */
export function defaultEnd(start: string): string {
  const startDate = parseLocalDateTime(start);
  return startDate ? localDateTime(new Date(startDate.getTime() + DEFAULT_LENGTH_MS)) : "";
}

/**
 * Keeps an end's length when the start moves in the editor (as the database does for older builds): a valid
 * end before the move stays the same length after it.
 */
export function shiftedEnd(previousStart: string, nextStart: string, end: string): string {
  const before = parseLocalDateTime(previousStart);
  const after = parseLocalDateTime(nextStart);
  const endDate = parseLocalDateTime(end);
  if (!before || !after || !endDate || endDate <= before) return end;
  return localDateTime(new Date(after.getTime() + (endDate.getTime() - before.getTime())));
}
```

`mobile/src/domain/eventDetails.ts`:

<!-- block: task4-eventDetails -->
```ts
import { localDay, shortDay } from "./schedule";

// UX3 Meeting details and Note to guests on the event page (spec "Event details", UX3-D5, UX3-D6).

export type MeetingDetailsView = { kind: "shown"; text: string } | { kind: "locked" } | { kind: "none" };

/**
 * What the event page shows for Meeting details. The organizer and Going or Maybe guests see the text (the
 * database only sends it to them); anyone else sees a locked line when some exist, and nothing otherwise.
 */
export function meetingDetailsView(view: {
  owner: boolean;
  response: string | null;
  hasDetails: boolean;
  text: string;
}): MeetingDetailsView {
  const insider = view.owner || view.response === "accepted" || view.response === "maybe";
  if (insider) return view.text.trim() ? { kind: "shown", text: view.text.trim() } : { kind: "none" };
  return view.hasDetails ? { kind: "locked" } : { kind: "none" };
}

export const MEETING_DETAILS_LOCKED = "Meeting details appear after you answer Going or Maybe.";
export const MEETING_DETAILS_HINT = "Only people who answer Going or Maybe see this.";

export const GUEST_NOTE_MAX = 280;

/** The note sheet's check: up to 280 characters after trimming. An empty note removes it. */
export function guestNoteError(text: string): string | null {
  return text.trim().length > GUEST_NOTE_MAX ? `Keep the note to ${GUEST_NOTE_MAX} characters.` : null;
}

/** The posted time on the note card: "9:41" today, "Fri 16 9:41" on another day, or "" when unknown. */
export function guestNoteTime(iso: string | null | undefined, now: Date): string {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const date = new Date(iso);
  if (!Number.isFinite(date.getTime())) return "";
  const time = `${date.getHours()}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  return localDay(date) === localDay(now) ? time : `${shortDay(date)} ${time}`;
}

/**
 * Whether the organizer may post or change the note: an active event until it ends (its end time, or an
 * hour after the start), as the database enforces.
 */
export function canPostGuestNote(plan: { status: string; starts_at: string; ends_at?: string | null }, now: number): boolean {
  if (plan.status !== "active") return false;
  const start = Date.parse(plan.starts_at);
  const end = plan.ends_at ? Date.parse(plan.ends_at) : start + 60 * 60 * 1000;
  return Number.isFinite(end) && now < end;
}
```

`mobile/src/domain/calendarEvent.ts`:

<!-- block: task4-calendarEvent -->
```ts
import { DEFAULT_LENGTH_MS } from "./eventTime";

// UX3 Add to calendar (spec "Calendar and reminders", UX3-D8). Resbite fills iPhone's own form; the person saves.

export type CalendarSource = { title: string; starts_at: string; ends_at?: string | null; place_label: string };

/** What the form is pre-filled with. Never the Meeting details or a link; no alert of its own (UX3-D8). */
export type CalendarEventDetails = {
  title: string;
  startDate: Date;
  endDate: Date;
  location: string;
  notes: string;
  alarms: [];
};

export const CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE = "Added from Resbite";
export const CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE = "Updated. Remove the earlier entry from your calendar.";

export function calendarEventDetails(plan: CalendarSource, update = false): CalendarEventDetails {
  const startDate = new Date(plan.starts_at);
  const end = plan.ends_at ? new Date(plan.ends_at) : null;
  const endDate = end && end > startDate ? end : new Date(startDate.getTime() + DEFAULT_LENGTH_MS);
  return {
    title: plan.title.trim() || "Resbite",
    startDate,
    endDate,
    location: plan.place_label.trim(),
    notes: update ? CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE : CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE,
    alarms: [],
  };
}

/** What this phone remembers about an added event, to notice a later change of time or place. */
export type CalendarRecord = { starts_at: string; ends_at: string | null; place_label: string };

export function calendarRecordOf(plan: CalendarSource): CalendarRecord {
  return { starts_at: plan.starts_at, ends_at: plan.ends_at ?? null, place_label: plan.place_label.trim() };
}

const sameInstant = (a: string | null, b: string | null) =>
  a === b || (a !== null && b !== null && Date.parse(a) === Date.parse(b));

/** "Update calendar" shows when an event added on this phone has a new start, end or place. */
export function needsCalendarUpdate(record: CalendarRecord | null | undefined, plan: CalendarSource): boolean {
  if (!record) return false;
  return (
    !sameInstant(record.starts_at, plan.starts_at) ||
    !sameInstant(record.ends_at, plan.ends_at ?? null) ||
    record.place_label !== plan.place_label.trim()
  );
}

/** Who sees the calendar chip: the organizer and Going or Maybe guests, on active events that haven't started. */
export function canAddToCalendar(
  plan: { status: string; starts_at: string },
  view: { owner: boolean; response: string | null },
  now: number,
): boolean {
  return (
    plan.status === "active" &&
    Date.parse(plan.starts_at) > now &&
    (view.owner || view.response === "accepted" || view.response === "maybe")
  );
}
```

`mobile/src/domain/reminders.ts`:

<!-- block: task4-reminders -->
```ts
// UX3 reminders (spec "Calendar and reminders", UX3-D4): two local notifications, 1 day and 1 hour before the
// start, for events the person organizes or answered Going or Maybe. Name and time only, never the place.

export type ReminderSource = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  starts_at: string;
  status: string;
  owner: boolean;
  response: string | null;
};
export type Reminder = { id: string; planId: string; at: Date; body: string };
export type ScheduledReminder = { id: string; at: number | null; body: string };

/** Every reminder identifier starts with this, so syncing never touches other notifications. */
export const REMINDER_PREFIX = "resbite-reminder:";
/** iOS keeps at most 64 pending local notifications per app; stay well under it, soonest first. */
export const REMINDER_LIMIT = 60;
const DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const HOUR = 60 * 60 * 1000;
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

export function remindersWanted(source: ReminderSource): boolean {
  return source.status === "active" && (source.owner || source.response === "accepted" || source.response === "maybe");
}

/** The reminders this phone should have: none when switched off; none for muted, past or other events. */
export function plannedReminders(
  sources: readonly ReminderSource[],
  options: { now: Date; enabled: boolean; muted: ReadonlySet<string> },
): Reminder[] {
  if (!options.enabled) return [];
  const now = options.now.getTime();
  const reminders: Reminder[] = [];
  for (const source of sources) {
    if (!remindersWanted(source) || options.muted.has(source.id)) continue;
    const start = new Date(source.starts_at);
    if (!Number.isFinite(start.getTime()) || start.getTime() <= now) continue;
    const title = source.title.trim() || "your resbite";
    const clock = `${pad(start.getHours())}:${pad(start.getMinutes())}`;
    const day = new Date(start.getTime() - DAY);
    const hour = new Date(start.getTime() - HOUR);
    if (day.getTime() > now)
      reminders.push({ id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}${source.id}:1d`, planId: source.id, at: day, body: `Tomorrow at ${clock} · ${title}` });
    if (hour.getTime() > now)
      reminders.push({ id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}${source.id}:1h`, planId: source.id, at: hour, body: `In 1 hour · ${title}` });
  }
  return reminders.sort((a, b) => a.at.getTime() - b.at.getTime()).slice(0, REMINDER_LIMIT);
}

/** What to cancel and what to schedule so the phone holds exactly `wanted` (only Resbite reminders are touched). */
export function reminderChanges(
  scheduled: readonly ScheduledReminder[],
  wanted: readonly Reminder[],
): { cancel: string[]; schedule: Reminder[] } {
  const ours = scheduled.filter((s) => s.id.startsWith(REMINDER_PREFIX));
  const want = new Map(wanted.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const cancel = ours.filter((s) => {
    const w = want.get(s.id);
    return !w || w.at.getTime() !== s.at || w.body !== s.body;
  });
  const kept = new Set(ours.filter((s) => !cancel.includes(s)).map((s) => s.id));
  return { cancel: cancel.map((s) => s.id), schedule: wanted.filter((r) => !kept.has(r.id)) };
}
```

### Task 5: Plan data: read and save the end time, Meeting details and the Note to guests

**Files:**
- Create: `mobile/src/services/guestNote.ts` (+ `guestNote.test.ts`)
- Modify: `mobile/src/domain/plans.ts` (+ `plans.test.ts`)
- Modify: `mobile/src/services/plans.ts`, `mobile/src/services/rsvp.ts`, `mobile/src/state/usePlanEditor.ts`
- Modify (fixtures gain `ends_at`): `mobile/src/domain/events.test.ts`, `mobile/src/domain/rules.test.ts`, `mobile/src/domain/eventView.test.ts`, `mobile/src/services/draftStore.test.ts`
- Modify (Supabase stubs answer the v3 functions and embed Meeting details): `mobile/scripts/verify-event.mjs`, `mobile/scripts/verify-planning.mjs`, `mobile/scripts/verify-covers.mjs`
- Modify: `mobile/package.json` (the `test` chain)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 1–2: `create_plan_v3` and `change_plan_v3` (`p_ends`, `p_note` = Meeting details), the `plan_meeting_details(note)` embed, `plans.ends_at`, `plans.has_meeting_details`, `plans.guest_note`, `plans.guest_note_at` and `set_guest_note(p_plan, p_note) returns jsonb`.
  - Task 4: `guestNoteError(text)`.
- Produces:
  - `Plan` (`src/domain/plans.ts`) gains `ends_at?: string | null`, `guest_note?: string`, `guest_note_at?: string | null` and `has_meeting_details?: boolean`. `Plan.note` now means the Meeting details this person may read (`""` otherwise).
  - `PlanRow` (a plans row with `plan_meeting_details` embedded) and `planFromRow(row: PlanRow): Plan`. Every plans read goes through `planFromRow`, because the row's own `note` column is always empty now.
  - `PlanDetails` gains `ends_at: string | null`. `samePlanDetails` compares ends as instants (both absent, or the same instant).
  - `src/services/plans.ts`:
    - `planWithDetails = "*,plan_meeting_details(note)"`, the select string for the event page and the editor;
    - the plan list selects `ends_at`, `has_meeting_details`, `guest_note`, `guest_note_at` and `plan_meeting_details(note)`;
    - create and change call `create_plan_v3` / `change_plan_v3` with `p_ends` (`null` when there is no end, and on cancel);
    - `setGuestNote(planId: string, text: string): Promise<GuestNote>`.
  - `src/services/guestNote.ts`: `GuestNote = { guest_note: string; guest_note_at: string | null }` and `createGuestNoteWriter(call)`. Its errors are "You can’t post a note on this resbite." (`42501`), "This resbite has ended or was cancelled, so its note can’t change." (`22023`) and "We couldn’t save the note. Check your connection and try again." (anything else, a lost reply, or a reply that isn't a note). It trims the text and refuses more than 280 characters before calling.
  - `src/services/rsvp.ts`: `readRsvp` and `readEvent` read `planWithDetails`, so answering Going or Maybe brings the Meeting details and Can’t go hides them on the next read.
  - `usePlanEditor` keeps an edited event's length (the end moves with the start) until Task 7 gives the When step its own end.

```diff
diff --git a/src/services/guestNote.test.ts b/src/services/guestNote.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..53786dd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/guestNote.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { createGuestNoteWriter } from "./guestNote.ts";
+
+const saved = { guest_note: "Running 10 minutes late", guest_note_at: "2099-01-01T14:50:00Z" };
+
+test("the note is sent trimmed and the saved note comes back with its time", async () => {
+  const sent: [string, string][] = [];
+  const setGuestNote = createGuestNoteWriter(async (planId, text) => {
+    sent.push([planId, text]);
+    return { data: saved, error: null };
+  });
+  assert.deepEqual(await setGuestNote("plan-1", "  Running 10 minutes late \n"), saved);
+  assert.deepEqual(sent, [["plan-1", "Running 10 minutes late"]]);
+});
+
+test("removing the note sends an empty text and returns no time", async () => {
+  const setGuestNote = createGuestNoteWriter(async () => ({
+    data: { guest_note: "", guest_note_at: null },
+    error: null,
+  }));
+  assert.deepEqual(await setGuestNote("plan-1", "   "), { guest_note: "", guest_note_at: null });
+});
+
+test("a note over 280 characters is refused before anything is sent", async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const setGuestNote = createGuestNoteWriter(async () => {
+    calls++;
+    return { data: saved, error: null };
+  });
+  await assert.rejects(setGuestNote("plan-1", "x".repeat(281)), /Keep the note to 280 characters\./);
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  await setGuestNote("plan-1", ` ${"x".repeat(280)} `);
+  assert.equal(calls, 1);
+});
+
+test("refusals and failures read as friendly errors", async () => {
+  const cases: [Parameters<typeof createGuestNoteWriter>[0], RegExp][] = [
+    [async () => ({ data: null, error: { code: "42501" } }), /You can’t post a note on this resbite\.$/],
+    [
+      async () => ({ data: null, error: { code: "22023" } }),
+      /This resbite has ended or was cancelled, so its note can’t change\.$/,
+    ],
+    [async () => ({ data: null, error: { code: "PGRST301" } }), /Check your connection and try again\.$/],
+    [async () => ({ data: null, error: {} }), /We couldn’t save the note\. Check your connection and try again\.$/],
+    [
+      async () => {
+        throw new Error("Network request failed");
+      },
+      /We couldn’t save the note\. Check your connection and try again\.$/,
+    ],
+    // An unexpected reply is never shown as saved.
+    [async () => ({ data: { guest_note: 7 }, error: null }), /Check your connection/],
+    [async () => ({ data: null, error: null }), /Check your connection/],
+  ];
+  for (const [reply, message] of cases)
+    await assert.rejects(createGuestNoteWriter(reply)("plan-1", "Hello"), message);
+});
```

```diff
+/** A plans row read with its Meeting details embedded as `plan_meeting_details(note)`. */
+export type PlanRow = Omit<Plan, "note"> & {
+  note?: string | null;
+  plan_meeting_details?: { note: string | null } | { note: string | null }[] | null;
+};
+
+/**
+ * The app's Plan from a plans row (UX3). Meeting details live in their own table, which the database only
+ * returns to the organizer and Going or Maybe guests; the row's own note column is always empty now.
+ */
+export function planFromRow(row: PlanRow): Plan {
+  const { plan_meeting_details: embedded, ...plan } = row;
+  const details = Array.isArray(embedded) ? embedded[0] : embedded;
+  return { ...plan, note: details?.note ?? "" };
+}

diff --git a/src/services/guestNote.ts b/src/services/guestNote.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ea48791
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/guestNote.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import { guestNoteError } from "../domain/eventDetails";
+
+export type GuestNote = { guest_note: string; guest_note_at: string | null };
+type Reply = { data: unknown; error: { code?: string } | null };
+
+const notYours = "You can’t post a note on this resbite.";
+const closed = "This resbite has ended or was cancelled, so its note can’t change.";
+const unsaved = "We couldn’t save the note. Check your connection and try again.";
+
+const isGuestNote = (value: unknown): value is GuestNote => {
+  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false;
+  const row = value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  return (
+    typeof row.guest_note === "string" &&
+    (row.guest_note_at === null || typeof row.guest_note_at === "string")
+  );
+};
+
+/**
+ * UX3 Note to guests, around the `set_guest_note` call. Saving the same text again is harmless (the database
+ * returns the saved note unchanged), so a retry after a lost reply is safe. The database refuses with 42501
+ * for anyone but the organizer and 22023 once the event has ended or was cancelled.
+ */
+export function createGuestNoteWriter(call: (planId: string, text: string) => PromiseLike<Reply>) {
+  return async function setGuestNote(planId: string, text: string): Promise<GuestNote> {
+    const invalid = guestNoteError(text);
+    if (invalid) throw Error(invalid);
+    let reply: Reply;
+    try {
+      reply = await call(planId, text.trim());
+    } catch {
+      throw Error(unsaved);
+    }
+    if (reply.error)
+      throw Error(
+        reply.error.code === "42501" ? notYours : reply.error.code === "22023" ? closed : unsaved,
+      );
+    if (!isGuestNote(reply.data)) throw Error(unsaved);
+    return { guest_note: reply.data.guest_note, guest_note_at: reply.data.guest_note_at };
+  };
+}

             original && Date.parse(original.starts_at) === date!.getTime()
```

### Task 6: Reminders and calendar on this phone

**Files:**
- Create: `mobile/src/services/reminders.ts` (+ `reminders.test.ts`): the reminder service, the sync and the expo-notifications adapter
- Create: `mobile/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.ts` (+ test): the reminder switches kept on this phone
- Create: `mobile/src/services/deviceReminders.ts`: the app's reminder entry points (native or fake)
- Create: `mobile/src/services/calendar.ts` (+ `calendar.test.ts`): the calendar service and the expo-calendar adapter
- Create: `mobile/src/services/calendarRecordStore.ts` (+ test): which events this phone added to the calendar
- Create: `mobile/src/services/deviceCalendar.ts`: the app's calendar entry points (native or fake)
- Create: `mobile/src/services/deviceMirror.ts`: the development-only `globalThis.__resbiteDevice` mirror
- Create: `mobile/src/state/useDeviceReminders.ts`: the root reminder hook
- Modify: `mobile/src/services/notifications.ts` (shares `permissionFrom`), `mobile/src/services/sessionLifecycle.ts` (+ test), `mobile/src/services/deletionLifecycle.test.ts`, `mobile/src/state/AppState.tsx`, `mobile/app/_layout.tsx`
- Modify: `mobile/app.json` (the `expo-calendar` plugin and `ios.associatedDomains`), `mobile/package.json` and `mobile/package-lock.json` (`expo-calendar`, the `test` chain)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 4: `plannedReminders`, `reminderChanges`, `remindersWanted`, `REMINDER_PREFIX`, `REMINDER_LIMIT`, `ReminderSource`, `Reminder`, `ScheduledReminder`, `calendarEventDetails`, `calendarRecordOf`, `CalendarSource`, `CalendarRecord`.
  - Task 5: `Plan.ends_at`.
- Produces:
  - `src/services/deviceReminders.ts`. A scope is `ReminderScope = { userId: string | null; preview: boolean; localPlans?: readonly ReminderPlanRow[] }`; preview passes its local plans and never reads the server. The functions:
    - `syncReminders(scope): Promise<void>`;
    - `reminderPermission(scope): Promise<NotificationPermission>`;
    - `askForReminders(scope): Promise<NotificationPermission>`, which shows iPhone's prompt only while unanswered, then syncs;
    - `remindersFor(scope): Promise<ReminderPrefs>`, with `ReminderPrefs = { enabled: boolean; muted: string[]; asked: boolean }`;
    - `setRemindersEnabled(scope, on, assertCurrent?)`, `muteReminders(scope, planId, muted, assertCurrent?)` and `markRemindersAsked(scope, assertCurrent?)`, each returning `Promise<ReminderPrefs>`;
    - `clearReminders(scopeKey: string)`;
    - `listenForReminders(open: (planId: string) => void): () => void`.
  - `src/services/reminders.ts`:
    - `remindersAllowed(permission)` (full or quiet permission);
    - `deviceScope({ userId, preview })`, which gives `"preview"`, the user id, or `null` when signed out;
    - `scheduledAt(request)`, which reads `data.at` (decision P11);
    - `reminderTapPlan(response): string | null`, the event a tapped Resbite reminder opens;
    - `reminderSources(plans, answers, who)`, `createReminderService(adapter)`, `createReminderSync(deps)`, `nativeReminderAdapter()` and `createFakeReminderAdapter()`.
    - Reminders use identifiers `resbite-reminder:<plan>:1d|1h`, the default sound (P9), and `data: { planId, at }`.
  - `src/services/deviceCalendar.ts`:
    - `addToCalendar(scope, plan: CalendarSource & { id: string }, update: boolean, assertCurrent?): Promise<boolean>` opens iPhone's own form, with no permission (P1, P10);
    - `calendarRecord(scope, planId): Promise<CalendarRecord | null>` and `clearCalendarRecords(scopeKey)`.
  - `deviceMirror(): DeviceMirror | null`, where `DeviceMirror = { reminders: { id; at; body }[]; calendar: CalendarEventDetails[]; permission: "undetermined" | "granted" | "denied" }`. It exists in development builds only; browser scripts read it and may set `permission`.
  - `useDeviceReminders(ready: boolean)`, called from `app/_layout.tsx`. It syncs on session changes and on return to the foreground, and opens the event when a reminder is tapped.
  - Sign-out and account deletion clear the scope's reminders (scheduled and shown, P12), its reminder switches and its calendar records, before the auth sign-out. A failed clear fails the sign-out.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install the calendar module.** From `mobile/`: `npx expo install expo-calendar`. Expected: `package.json` gains `"expo-calendar": "~57.0.5"` (or the 57.0 patch Expo picks) and `package-lock.json` changes.
  - If `git diff --stat` also shows `app.json` (Expo may add a bare `"expo-calendar"` plugin entry), run `git checkout -- app.json`. Step 4's patch adds the plugin with its purpose strings.
  - Don't change any other dependency.

```diff
diff --git a/src/services/calendar.test.ts b/src/services/calendar.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..030dd11
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/calendar.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,166 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import ts from "typescript";
+import * as calendarDomain from "../domain/calendarEvent.ts";
+import * as mirrorModule from "./deviceMirror.ts";
+import { CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE, CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE, type CalendarEventDetails } from "../domain/calendarEvent.ts";
+import type { DeviceMirror } from "./deviceMirror.ts";
+import { createCalendarRecordStore } from "./calendarRecordStore.ts";
+import {
+  createCalendarService,
+  createFakeCalendarAdapter,
+  type CalendarAdapter,
+  type CalendarResult,
+} from "./calendar.ts";
+
+function memory() {
+  const data = new Map<string, string>();
+  return {
+    data,
+    getItem: async (k: string) => data.get(k) ?? null,
+    setItem: async (k: string, v: string) => {
+      data.set(k, v);
+    },
+    removeItem: async (k: string) => {
+      data.delete(k);
+    },
+  };
+}
+const plan = {
+  id: "plan-1",
+  title: "Picnic in the park",
+  starts_at: "2099-01-01T10:00:00Z",
+  ends_at: null,
+  place_label: " Riverside park ",
+  note: "Door code 4521",
+};
+
+function harness(result: CalendarResult) {
+  const shown: CalendarEventDetails[] = [];
+  const adapter: CalendarAdapter = {
+    add: async (details) => {
+      shown.push(details);
+      return result;
+    },
+  };
+  const records = createCalendarRecordStore(memory());
+  return { shown, records, calendar: createCalendarService(adapter, records) };
+}
+
+test("a saved entry is remembered with the start, end and place it was added with", async () => {
+  for (const result of ["saved", "done"] as const) {
+    const h = harness(result);
+    assert.equal(await h.calendar.add("owner", plan, false), true);
+    assert.deepEqual(await h.records.get("owner", "plan-1"), {
+      starts_at: plan.starts_at,
+      ends_at: null,
+      place_label: "Riverside park",
+    });
+    // The form gets the name, times and place; never the Meeting details, and no alert.
+    assert.deepEqual(h.shown, [
+      {
+        title: "Picnic in the park",
+        startDate: new Date("2099-01-01T10:00:00Z"),
+        endDate: new Date("2099-01-01T11:00:00Z"),
+        location: "Riverside park",
+        notes: CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE,
+        alarms: [],
+      },
+    ]);
+  }
+});
+
+test("a form that can't open (iOS 16, or one already open) rejects for the page to explain, and remembers nothing", async () => {
+  // Controller ruling P10: the page shows CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED; Resbite never asks for calendar access.
+  const records = createCalendarRecordStore(memory());
+  const failing: CalendarAdapter = {
+    add: async () => {
+      throw Error("Calendar permissions not granted");
+    },
+  };
+  await assert.rejects(createCalendarService(failing, records).add("owner", plan, false), /permissions not granted/);
+  assert.equal(await records.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+});
+
+test("a cancelled form remembers nothing; Update calendar fills in the updated note", async () => {
+  for (const result of ["canceled", "deleted"] as const) {
+    const h = harness(result);
+    assert.equal(await h.calendar.add("owner", plan, true), false);
+    assert.equal(await h.records.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+    assert.equal(h.shown[0].notes, CALENDAR_UPDATED_NOTE);
+  }
+});
+
+test("the entry counts as added even when this phone can't remember it", async () => {
+  const records = { remember: async () => Promise.reject(Error("locked")) };
+  const calendar = createCalendarService({ add: async () => "saved" }, records);
+  assert.equal(await calendar.add("owner", plan, false), true);
+  const changed = createCalendarService({ add: async () => "saved" }, createCalendarRecordStore(memory()));
+  assert.equal(
+    await changed.add("owner", plan, false, () => {
+      throw Error("Session changed. Please try again.");
+    }),
+    true,
+  );
+});
+
+test("the web and preview fake saves, and shows the entry to browser QA in development only", async () => {
+  const device: DeviceMirror = { reminders: [], calendar: [], permission: "undetermined" };
+  assert.equal(await createFakeCalendarAdapter(device).add(sample()), "saved");
+  assert.equal(device.calendar.length, 1);
+  assert.equal(device.calendar[0].title, "Picnic in the park");
+  // Without a mirror (a release build), nothing is recorded anywhere.
+  assert.equal(await createFakeCalendarAdapter(null).add(sample()), "saved");
+  assert.equal(globalThis.__resbiteDevice, undefined);
+});
+
+function sample(): CalendarEventDetails {
+  return {
+    title: "Picnic in the park",
+    startDate: new Date("2099-01-01T10:00:00Z"),
+    endDate: new Date("2099-01-01T11:00:00Z"),
+    location: "Riverside park",
+    notes: CALENDAR_ADDED_NOTE,
+    alarms: [],
+  };
+}
+
+// The real phone's boundary, run against a stand-in for expo-calendar's legacy API (as nativeImports.test.ts).
+test("the phone opens iPhone's own form with the details, and only saved or done counts as added", async () => {
+  const opened: unknown[] = [];
+  let action = "saved";
+  const source = readFileSync(new URL("./calendar.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
+  const compiled = ts.transpileModule(source, {
+    compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, esModuleInterop: true },
+  }).outputText;
+  const exports: Record<string, any> = {};
+  runInNewContext(compiled, {
+    exports,
+    require: (name: string) => {
+      if (name === "../domain/calendarEvent") return calendarDomain;
+      if (name === "./deviceMirror") return mirrorModule;
+      if (name === "expo-calendar/legacy")
+        return {
+          createEventInCalendarAsync: async (details: unknown, options?: unknown) => {
+            opened.push([details, options]);
+            return { action, id: null };
+          },
+        };
+      throw Error(`Unexpected import: ${name}`);
+    },
+  });
+  const adapter: CalendarAdapter = exports.nativeCalendarAdapter();
+  assert.equal(await adapter.add(sample()), "saved");
+  assert.deepEqual(opened, [[sample(), undefined]]);
+  for (const [reported, expected] of [
+    ["done", "done"],
+    ["canceled", "canceled"],
+    ["deleted", "deleted"],
+    ["responded", "canceled"],
+  ]) {
+    action = reported;
+    assert.equal(await adapter.add(sample()), expected);
+  }
+});
diff --git a/src/services/calendarRecordStore.test.ts b/src/services/calendarRecordStore.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fa1ff9e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/calendarRecordStore.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX, createCalendarRecordStore } from "./calendarRecordStore.ts";
+
+function memory() {
+  const data = new Map<string, string>();
+  return {
+    data,
+    getItem: async (k: string) => data.get(k) ?? null,
+    setItem: async (k: string, v: string) => {
+      data.set(k, v);
+    },
+    removeItem: async (k: string) => {
+      data.delete(k);
+    },
+  };
+}
+const record = { starts_at: "2099-01-01T10:00:00Z", ends_at: null, place_label: "Riverside park" };
+
+test("an added event is remembered per scope with what it was added with; clear removes only that scope", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createCalendarRecordStore(disk);
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+  await store.remember("owner", "plan-1", record);
+  await store.remember("preview", "plan-9", record);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), record);
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-9"), null);
+  assert.deepEqual([...disk.data.keys()].sort(), [
+    "resbite.calendar-records.v1.owner",
+    "resbite.calendar-records.v1.preview",
+  ]);
+  await store.clear("owner");
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("preview", "plan-9"), record);
+});
+
+test("adding again (Update calendar) replaces the record, and the oldest drop off past the limit", async () => {
+  const store = createCalendarRecordStore(memory());
+  await store.remember("owner", "plan-1", record);
+  const moved = { starts_at: "2099-01-02T10:00:00Z", ends_at: "2099-01-02T12:00:00Z", place_label: "Old mill" };
+  await store.remember("owner", "plan-1", moved);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), moved);
+  for (let i = 0; i < CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX; i++) await store.remember("owner", `p${i}`, record);
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", `p${CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX - 1}`), record);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", "p0"), record);
+});
+
+test("unreadable or invalid records read as not added, and the next add replaces them", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createCalendarRecordStore(disk);
+  const key = "resbite.calendar-records.v1.owner";
+  disk.data.set(key, "{not json");
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+  await store.remember("owner", "plan-1", record);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), record);
+  disk.data.set(
+    key,
+    JSON.stringify([{ planId: "plan-1", starts_at: 7 }, { planId: "plan-2", ...record, ends_at: undefined }, { planId: "plan-3", ...record }]),
+  );
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-1"), null);
+  assert.equal(await store.get("owner", "plan-2"), null);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.get("owner", "plan-3"), record);
+  const broken = createCalendarRecordStore({ ...disk, getItem: async () => Promise.reject(Error("locked")) });
+  assert.equal(await broken.get("owner", "plan-3"), null);
+});
+
+test("a session change before the write keeps the record off the device", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createCalendarRecordStore(disk);
+  await assert.rejects(
+    store.remember("owner", "plan-1", record, () => {
+      throw Error("Session changed. Please try again.");
+    }),
+    /Session changed/,
+  );
+  assert.equal(disk.data.size, 0);
+});

diff --git a/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.test.ts b/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2628cc0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,91 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { createReminderPrefsStore, MUTED_MAX } from "./reminderPrefsStore.ts";
+
+function memory() {
+  const data = new Map<string, string>();
+  return {
+    data,
+    getItem: async (k: string) => data.get(k) ?? null,
+    setItem: async (k: string, v: string) => {
+      data.set(k, v);
+    },
+    removeItem: async (k: string) => {
+      data.delete(k);
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+test("reminders start on, with nothing muted and the sheet not shown yet", async () => {
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore(memory());
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+});
+
+test("the switches are saved per scope and clear removes only that scope", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore(disk);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.setEnabled("owner", false), { enabled: false, muted: [], asked: false });
+  await store.setMuted("owner", "plan-1", true);
+  await store.markAsked("owner");
+  await store.setMuted("preview", "plan-9", true);
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: false, muted: ["plan-1"], asked: true });
+  assert.deepEqual([...disk.data.keys()].sort(), [
+    "resbite.reminder-prefs.v1.owner",
+    "resbite.reminder-prefs.v1.preview",
+  ]);
+  await store.clear("owner");
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+  assert.deepEqual((await store.read("preview")).muted, ["plan-9"]);
+});
+
+test("muting is per event, never duplicated, and unmuting removes it", async () => {
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore(memory());
+  await store.setMuted("owner", "plan-1", true);
+  await store.setMuted("owner", "plan-2", true);
+  await store.setMuted("owner", "plan-1", true);
+  assert.deepEqual((await store.read("owner")).muted, ["plan-2", "plan-1"]);
+  assert.deepEqual((await store.setMuted("owner", "plan-2", false)).muted, ["plan-1"]);
+  for (let i = 0; i < MUTED_MAX + 5; i++) await store.setMuted("owner", `p${i}`, true);
+  const { muted } = await store.read("owner");
+  assert.equal(muted.length, MUTED_MAX);
+  assert.equal(muted.at(-1), `p${MUTED_MAX + 4}`);
+  assert.equal(muted.includes("plan-1"), false);
+});
+
+test("unreadable or partly invalid data reads as the defaults, field by field", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore(disk);
+  const key = "resbite.reminder-prefs.v1.owner";
+  for (const raw of ["{not json", "null", "7", "[]"]) {
+    disk.data.set(key, raw);
+    assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+  }
+  disk.data.set(key, JSON.stringify({ enabled: "no", muted: ["a", 3, "", "a", "b"], asked: "yes" }));
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: true, muted: ["a", "b"], asked: false });
+  disk.data.set(key, "{not json");
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.setEnabled("owner", false), { enabled: false, muted: [], asked: false });
+});
+
+test("a storage failure reads as the defaults, but a failed save is reported", async () => {
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore({
+    getItem: async () => {
+      throw Error("locked");
+    },
+    setItem: async () => {},
+    removeItem: async () => {},
+  });
+  assert.deepEqual(await store.read("owner"), { enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+  await assert.rejects(store.setEnabled("owner", false), /locked/);
+});
+
+test("a session change before the write keeps the switch off the device", async () => {
+  const disk = memory();
+  const store = createReminderPrefsStore(disk);
+  await assert.rejects(
+    store.setMuted("owner", "plan-1", true, () => {
+      throw Error("Session changed. Please try again.");
+    }),
+    /Session changed/,
+  );
+  assert.equal(disk.data.size, 0);
+});
diff --git a/src/services/reminders.test.ts b/src/services/reminders.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1c0bd9c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/reminders.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,458 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { runInNewContext } from "node:vm";
+import ts from "typescript";
+import * as domain from "../domain/reminders.ts";
+import * as notificationsService from "./notifications.ts";
+import * as mirrorModule from "./deviceMirror.ts";
+import { REMINDER_PREFIX, type Reminder, type ReminderSource } from "../domain/reminders.ts";
+import type { DeviceMirror } from "./deviceMirror.ts";
+import type { ReminderPrefs } from "./reminderPrefsStore.ts";
+import {
+  createFakeReminderAdapter,
+  createReminderService,
+  createReminderSync,
+  reminderSources,
+  reminderTapPlan,
+  scheduledAt,
+  type ReminderAdapter,
+  type ReminderService,
+} from "./reminders.ts";
+
+const reminder = (id: string, at: number, body = "In 1 hour · Picnic"): Reminder => ({
+  id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}${id}`,
+  planId: id.split(":")[0],
+  at: new Date(at),
+  body,
+});
+const mirror = (): DeviceMirror => ({ reminders: [], calendar: [], permission: "undetermined" });
+
+function recordingAdapter(base = createFakeReminderAdapter(null)) {
+  const calls: string[] = [];
+  const adapter: ReminderAdapter = {
+    ...base,
+    cancel: async (id) => {
+      calls.push(`cancel:${id}`);
+      await base.cancel(id);
+    },
+    schedule: async (r) => {
+      calls.push(`schedule:${r.id}`);
+      await base.schedule(r);
+    },
+  };
+  return { adapter, calls };
+}
+
+test("sync schedules what is missing, moves what changed and removes the rest; other notifications stay", async () => {
+  const { adapter, calls } = recordingAdapter();
+  const others = { id: "someone-else", at: 5, body: "Not ours" };
+  const scheduled = adapter.scheduled;
+  adapter.scheduled = async () => [...(await scheduled()), others];
+  const service = createReminderService(adapter);
+  await service.sync([reminder("a:1d", 1000), reminder("b:1h", 2000)]);
+  calls.length = 0;
+  await service.sync([reminder("a:1d", 1000), reminder("b:1h", 3000), reminder("c:1h", 4000)]);
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [
+    `cancel:${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`,
+    `schedule:${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`,
+    `schedule:${REMINDER_PREFIX}c:1h`,
+  ]);
+  calls.length = 0;
+  await service.sync([]);
+  assert.deepEqual(calls.sort(), [
+    `cancel:${REMINDER_PREFIX}a:1d`,
+    `cancel:${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`,
+    `cancel:${REMINDER_PREFIX}c:1h`,
+  ]);
+});
+
+test("one failed change doesn't stop the others, and the sync reports it", async () => {
+  const { adapter, calls } = recordingAdapter();
+  adapter.schedule = async (r) => {
+    calls.push(`schedule:${r.id}`);
+    if (r.id.endsWith("a:1d")) throw Error("time interval must be greater than 0");
+  };
+  await assert.rejects(
+    createReminderService(adapter).sync([reminder("a:1d", 1000), reminder("b:1h", 2000)]),
+    /Some reminders couldn’t be updated/,
+  );
+  assert.equal(calls.length, 2);
+});
+
+test("clearAll removes every Resbite reminder, scheduled or already shown, and nothing else", async () => {
+  const removed: string[] = [];
+  const service = createReminderService({
+    ...createFakeReminderAdapter(null),
+    scheduled: async () => [
+      { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}a:1d`, at: 1, body: "" },
+      { id: "other", at: 1, body: "" },
+    ],
+    cancel: async (id) => {
+      removed.push(`cancel:${id}`);
+    },
+    delivered: async () => [`${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`, "push-1"],
+    dismiss: async (id) => {
+      removed.push(`dismiss:${id}`);
+    },
+  });
+  await service.clearAll();
+  assert.deepEqual(removed, [`cancel:${REMINDER_PREFIX}a:1d`, `dismiss:${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`]);
+});
+
+test("permission reads iOS's richer status; the fake starts unanswered and the first request allows it", async () => {
+  const ios = createReminderService({
+    ...createFakeReminderAdapter(null),
+    platform: "ios",
+    getPermission: async () => ({ status: "denied", ios: { status: 3 } }),
+  });
+  assert.equal(await ios.permission(), "provisional");
+  const device = mirror();
+  const fake = createReminderService(createFakeReminderAdapter(device));
+  assert.equal(await fake.permission(), "undetermined");
+  assert.equal(await fake.request(), "granted");
+  assert.equal(device.permission, "granted");
+  // A QA script can turn it off; a request then leaves it off, as iPhone would.
+  device.permission = "denied";
+  assert.equal(await fake.permission(), "denied");
+  assert.equal(await fake.request(), "denied");
+});
+
+test("the fake mirrors its reminders for browser QA, soonest first, and nothing without a mirror", async () => {
+  const device = mirror();
+  const service = createReminderService(createFakeReminderAdapter(device));
+  await service.sync([reminder("b:1h", Date.UTC(2099, 0, 2)), reminder("a:1d", Date.UTC(2099, 0, 1))]);
+  assert.deepEqual(device.reminders, [
+    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}a:1d`, at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", body: "In 1 hour · Picnic" },
+    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}b:1h`, at: "2099-01-02T00:00:00.000Z", body: "In 1 hour · Picnic" },
+  ]);
+  await service.clearAll();
+  assert.deepEqual(device.reminders, []);
+  // Node has no __DEV__, as in a release build: there is no mirror at all.
+  assert.equal(mirrorModule.deviceMirror(), null);
+  assert.equal(globalThis.__resbiteDevice, undefined);
+});
+
+test("a scheduled reminder's time comes from its data, or Android's date trigger", () => {
+  const at = Date.UTC(2099, 0, 1, 9);
+  // iOS reports a date trigger as a time interval from when it was scheduled.
+  const ios = { content: { data: { planId: "a", at } }, trigger: { type: "timeInterval", seconds: 3600, repeats: false } };
+  assert.equal(scheduledAt(ios), at);
+  assert.equal(scheduledAt({ content: { data: {} }, trigger: { type: "date", value: at, repeats: false } }), at);
+  assert.equal(scheduledAt({ content: {}, trigger: { type: "timeInterval", seconds: 3600 } }), null);
+  assert.equal(scheduledAt({ content: { data: null }, trigger: null }), null);
+});
+
+test("only a tapped Resbite reminder with a plan id opens an event", () => {
+  const response = (identifier: string, data: Record<string, unknown> | undefined) => ({
+    notification: { request: { identifier, content: { data } } },
+  });
+  assert.equal(reminderTapPlan(response(`${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`, { planId: "p1", at: 1 })), "p1");
+  assert.equal(reminderTapPlan(response("push-1", { planId: "p1" })), null);
+  assert.equal(reminderTapPlan(response(`${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`, { planId: 7 })), null);
+  assert.equal(reminderTapPlan(response(`${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`, undefined)), null);
+  assert.equal(reminderTapPlan(null), null);
+});
+
+test("sources mark the person's own events and their own answers", () => {
+  const plans = [
+    { id: "own", title: "Picnic", starts_at: "2099-01-01T10:00:00Z", status: "active", owner_id: "me" },
+    { id: "invited", title: "Walk", starts_at: "2099-01-02T10:00:00Z", status: "active", owner_id: "host" },
+    { id: "unanswered", title: "Talk", starts_at: "2099-01-03T10:00:00Z", status: "active", owner_id: "host" },
+  ];
+  const answers = [{ plan_id: "invited", response: "maybe" }];
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    reminderSources(plans, answers, { userId: "me", preview: false }).map((s) => [s.id, s.owner, s.response]),
+    [
+      ["own", true, null],
+      ["invited", false, "maybe"],
+      ["unanswered", false, null],
+    ],
+  );
+  // Preview's plans on the device carry no owner; they are all the person's own.
+  const local = plans.map(({ owner_id: _owner, ...plan }) => plan);
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    reminderSources(local, [], { userId: null, preview: true }).map((s) => s.owner),
+    [true, true, true],
+  );
+});
+
+function deferred<T = void>() {
+  let resolve!: (value: T) => void;
+  const promise = new Promise<T>((r) => {
+    resolve = r;
+  });
+  return { promise, resolve };
+}
+
+const NOW = new Date(Date.UTC(2099, 0, 1, 8));
+const source = (id: string, hoursAhead: number, extra: Partial<ReminderSource> = {}): ReminderSource => ({
+  id,
+  title: id,
+  starts_at: new Date(NOW.getTime() + hoursAhead * 3600_000).toISOString(),
+  status: "active",
+  owner: true,
+  response: null,
+  ...extra,
+});
+
+function syncHarness(options: { permission?: string; prefs?: Partial<ReminderPrefs> } = {}) {
+  const phone = mirror();
+  phone.permission = (options.permission ?? "granted") as DeviceMirror["permission"];
+  const previewPhone = mirror();
+  previewPhone.permission = "granted";
+  const recorded = recordingAdapter(createFakeReminderAdapter(phone));
+  const services: Record<string, ReminderService> = {
+    phone: createReminderService(recorded.adapter),
+    preview: createReminderService(createFakeReminderAdapter(previewPhone)),
+  };
+  let sources: () => Promise<ReminderSource[]> = async () => [source("p1", 48)];
+  const reads: string[] = [];
+  const prefs: ReminderPrefs = { enabled: true, muted: [], asked: true, ...options.prefs };
+  const sync = createReminderSync({
+    service: (preview) => services[preview ? "preview" : "phone"],
+    prefs: async (scope) => {
+      reads.push(`prefs:${scope}`);
+      return prefs;
+    },
+    readSources: async (userId) => {
+      reads.push(`plans:${userId}`);
+      return sources();
+    },
+    now: () => NOW,
+  });
+  return {
+    sync,
+    phone,
+    previewPhone,
+    reads,
+    prefs,
+    /** Every cancel and schedule that reached the phone. */
+    changes: recorded.calls,
+    setSources: (fn: () => Promise<ReminderSource[]>) => {
+      sources = fn;
+    },
+    ids: (device = phone) => device.reminders.map((r) => r.id.slice(REMINDER_PREFIX.length)),
+  };
+}
+
+test("a sync sets the two reminders for the person's events, honouring the switches", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  h.setSources(async () => [
+    source("own", 48),
+    source("going", 30, { owner: false, response: "accepted" }),
+    source("cant", 30, { owner: false, response: "declined" }),
+    source("muted", 30),
+  ]);
+  h.prefs.muted = ["muted"];
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), ["going:1d", "own:1d", "going:1h", "own:1h"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.reads, ["prefs:me", "plans:me"]);
+  h.prefs.enabled = false;
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), []);
+});
+
+test("without permission nothing is scheduled, nothing is read and nothing is asked", async () => {
+  for (const permission of ["undetermined", "denied"]) {
+    const h = syncHarness({ permission });
+    await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+    assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), []);
+    assert.deepEqual(h.reads, []);
+    assert.equal(h.phone.permission, permission);
+  }
+});
+
+test("a failed read leaves the phone's reminders as they were", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), ["p1:1d", "p1:1h"]);
+  h.setSources(async () => {
+    throw Error("offline");
+  });
+  await assert.rejects(h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false }), /offline/);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), ["p1:1d", "p1:1h"]);
+});
+
+test("signed out, the phone keeps no reminders", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: null, preview: false });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.reads, ["prefs:me", "plans:me"]);
+});
+
+test("preview syncs its own plans on its fake, and reuses them when a call passes none", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  const localPlans = [{ id: "local", title: "Picnic", starts_at: source("x", 48).starts_at, status: "active" }];
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: true, localPlans });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(h.previewPhone), ["local:1d", "local:1h"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.reads, ["prefs:preview"]);
+  h.prefs.muted = ["local"];
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: true });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(h.previewPhone), []);
+  h.prefs.muted = [];
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: true });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(h.previewPhone), ["local:1d", "local:1h"]);
+  await h.sync.clear("preview");
+  await h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: true });
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(h.previewPhone), []);
+});
+
+test("a newer sync supersedes an older one that hasn't reached the phone", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  const slow = deferred<ReminderSource[]>();
+  h.setSources(() => slow.promise);
+  const older = h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
+  h.setSources(async () => [source("newer", 48)]);
+  const newer = h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  slow.resolve([source("older", 48)]);
+  await Promise.all([older, newer]);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), ["newer:1d", "newer:1h"]);
+  assert.equal(h.changes.some((change) => change.includes("older")), false);
+});
+
+test("sign-out clears after a sync in flight, and that sync can't add reminders back", async () => {
+  const h = syncHarness();
+  const slow = deferred<ReminderSource[]>();
+  h.setSources(() => slow.promise);
+  const inFlight = h.sync.sync({ userId: "me", preview: false });
+  await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
+  const cleared = h.sync.clear("me");
+  slow.resolve([source("p1", 48)]);
+  await Promise.all([inFlight, cleared]);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.ids(), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(h.changes, []);
+});
+
+// The real phone's boundary, run against a recording stand-in for expo-notifications (as nativeImports.test.ts).
+function loadNative() {
+  const calls: unknown[][] = [];
+  const at = Date.UTC(2099, 0, 1, 9);
+  const notifications = {
+    SchedulableTriggerInputTypes: { DATE: "date" },
+    getPermissionsAsync: async () => ({ status: "granted", ios: { status: 2 } }),
+    requestPermissionsAsync: async (request: unknown) => {
+      calls.push(["request", request]);
+      return { status: "granted", ios: { status: 3 } };
+    },
+    getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync: async () => [
+      {
+        identifier: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`,
+        content: { body: "In 1 hour · Picnic", data: { planId: "p1", at } },
+        trigger: { type: "timeInterval", seconds: 3600, repeats: false },
+      },
+    ],
+    scheduleNotificationAsync: async (request: unknown) => {
+      calls.push(["schedule", request]);
+      return "id";
+    },
+    cancelScheduledNotificationAsync: async (id: string) => {
+      calls.push(["cancel", id]);
+    },
+    getPresentedNotificationsAsync: async () => [{ request: { identifier: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p0:1h` } }],
+    dismissNotificationAsync: async (id: string) => {
+      calls.push(["dismiss", id]);
+    },
+  };
+  const source = readFileSync(new URL("./reminders.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
+  const compiled = ts.transpileModule(source, {
+    compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, esModuleInterop: true },
+  }).outputText;
+  const exports: Record<string, any> = {};
+  runInNewContext(compiled, {
+    exports,
+    require: (name: string) => {
+      if (name === "../domain/reminders") return domain;
+      if (name === "./notifications") return notificationsService;
+      if (name === "./deviceMirror") return mirrorModule;
+      if (name === "react-native") return { Platform: { OS: "ios" } };
+      if (name === "expo-notifications") return notifications;
+      throw Error(`Unexpected import: ${name}`);
+    },
+  });
+  return { adapter: exports.nativeReminderAdapter() as ReminderAdapter, calls, at };
+}
+
+test("the phone gets a dated reminder with the default sound and no badge, carrying its plan and time, and reads it back", async () => {
+  const { adapter, calls, at } = loadNative();
+  const service = createReminderService(adapter);
+  assert.equal(await service.request(), "provisional");
+  await service.sync([
+    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`, planId: "p1", at: new Date(at), body: "In 1 hour · Picnic" },
+    { id: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p2:1d`, planId: "p2", at: new Date(at + 1000), body: "Tomorrow at 10:00 · Walk" },
+  ]);
+  await service.clearAll();
+  // Objects made in the module's own context: compare them as plain data.
+  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(calls)), [
+    ["request", { ios: { allowAlert: true, allowSound: true, allowBadge: false } }],
+    // p1:1h is already on the phone with the same time and text, so only p2 is scheduled.
+    [
+      "schedule",
+      {
+        identifier: `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p2:1d`,
+        content: { body: "Tomorrow at 10:00 · Walk", sound: "default", data: { planId: "p2", at: at + 1000 } },
+        trigger: { type: "date", date: new Date(at + 1000).toISOString() },
+      },
+    ],
+    ["cancel", `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`],
+    ["dismiss", `${REMINDER_PREFIX}p0:1h`],
+  ]);
+});
+
+// The root layout's foreground handler (deviceReminders.ts), run with recording stand-ins for its imports.
+test("while Resbite is open, a reminder shows as a banner without sound or badge; other notifications stay hidden", async () => {
+  const calls: unknown[][] = [];
+  let handler: { handleNotification: (n: unknown) => Promise<Record<string, boolean>> } | null = null;
+  const notifications = {
+    setNotificationHandler: (next: typeof handler) => {
+      handler = next;
+    },
+    getLastNotificationResponse: () => null,
+    clearLastNotificationResponse: () => calls.push(["clear"]),
+    addNotificationResponseReceivedListener: () => ({ remove: () => calls.push(["remove"]) }),
+  };
+  const source = readFileSync(new URL("./deviceReminders.ts", import.meta.url), "utf8");
+  const compiled = ts.transpileModule(source, {
+    compilerOptions: { target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022, module: ts.ModuleKind.CommonJS, esModuleInterop: true },
+  }).outputText;
+  const exports: Record<string, any> = {};
+  runInNewContext(compiled, {
+    exports,
+    require: (name: string) => {
+      if (name === "react-native") return { Platform: { OS: "ios" } };
+      if (name === "expo-notifications") return notifications;
+      if (name === "../domain/reminders") return domain;
+      if (name === "./supabase") return { storage: {}, supabase: {} };
+      if (name === "./plans") return { withDeadline: () => undefined };
+      if (name === "./reminderPrefsStore") return { createReminderPrefsStore: () => ({}) };
+      if (name === "./reminders")
+        return {
+          createFakeReminderAdapter: () => ({}),
+          createReminderService: () => ({}),
+          createReminderSync: () => ({}),
+          reminderTapPlan,
+        };
+      throw Error(`Unexpected import: ${name}`);
+    },
+  });
+  const stop = exports.listenForReminders(() => {});
+  const show = (identifier: string) => handler!.handleNotification({ request: { identifier, content: {} } });
+  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(await show(`${REMINDER_PREFIX}p1:1h`))), {
+    shouldShowBanner: true,
+    shouldShowList: true,
+    shouldPlaySound: false,
+    shouldSetBadge: false,
+  });
+  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(await show("someone-else"))), {
+    shouldShowBanner: false,
+    shouldShowList: false,
+    shouldPlaySound: false,
+    shouldSetBadge: false,
+  });
+  stop();
+  assert.equal(handler, null);
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [["remove"]]);
+});

+      busy: () => {},

+    life.update({ account: "owner", preview: false });
+    await assert.rejects(life.signOut(), /disk/);
+    assert.deepEqual(events, failing === "reminders" ? ["reminders"] : ["reminders", "calendar"]);
+  }
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/services/calendar.ts b/src/services/calendar.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7ac1f05
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/calendar.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+import {
+  calendarEventDetails,
+  calendarRecordOf,
+  type CalendarEventDetails,
+  type CalendarRecord,
+  type CalendarSource,
+} from "../domain/calendarEvent";
+import { deviceMirror, type DeviceMirror } from "./deviceMirror";
+
+// UX3 Add to calendar (spec "Calendar and reminders", plan decision P1): iPhone's own add-event form,
+// opened with the details filled in. EventKitUI saves the event itself, so Resbite asks for no calendar
+// permission and can't read, change or remove anything in the calendar.
+
+/** How the form closed: "saved" or "canceled" (or "deleted") on iOS; Android only reports "done". */
+export type CalendarResult = "saved" | "canceled" | "done" | "deleted";
+export type CalendarAdapter = { add(details: CalendarEventDetails): Promise<CalendarResult> };
+type Records = {
+  remember(scope: string, planId: string, record: CalendarRecord, assertCurrent?: () => void): Promise<void>;
+};
+
+export function createCalendarService(adapter: CalendarAdapter, records: Records) {
+  return {
+    /**
+     * Opens the form for the event ("Update calendar" fills in the updated note). Returns whether it was
+     * added. What was added is remembered on this phone; that's a convenience, so it never fails the add.
+     */
+    async add(
+      scope: string,
+      plan: CalendarSource & { id: string },
+      update: boolean,
+      assertCurrent?: () => void,
+    ): Promise<boolean> {
+      const result = await adapter.add(calendarEventDetails(plan, update));
+      if (result !== "saved" && result !== "done") return false;
+      await records.remember(scope, plan.id, calendarRecordOf(plan), assertCurrent).catch(() => {});
+      return true;
+    },
+  };
+}
+export type CalendarService = ReturnType<typeof createCalendarService>;
+
+/** The real phone: iPhone's own form, with no permission prompt (expo-calendar's legacy API). */
+export function nativeCalendarAdapter(): CalendarAdapter {
+  return {
+    async add(details) {
+      const calendar = require("expo-calendar/legacy") as typeof import("expo-calendar/legacy");
+      const { action } = await calendar.createEventInCalendarAsync(details);
+      // Anything else the library may report counts as not added.
+      return action === "saved" || action === "done" || action === "deleted" ? action : "canceled";
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+/** Web and preview: nothing opens; the entry counts as saved. Development builds show it on `__resbiteDevice`. */
+export function createFakeCalendarAdapter(mirror: DeviceMirror | null = deviceMirror()): CalendarAdapter {
+  return {
+    async add(details) {
+      mirror?.calendar.push(details);
+      return "saved";
+    },
+  };
+}
diff --git a/src/services/calendarRecordStore.ts b/src/services/calendarRecordStore.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..645411a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/calendarRecordStore.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+import type { CalendarRecord } from "../domain/calendarEvent";
+import type { KeyStore } from "./secureChunks";
+
+/** The most added events remembered; the oldest addition drops off first. */
+export const CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX = 100;
+type Entry = CalendarRecord & { planId: string };
+
+const isEntry = (value: unknown): value is Entry => {
+  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false;
+  const e = value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  return (
+    typeof e.planId === "string" &&
+    e.planId !== "" &&
+    typeof e.starts_at === "string" &&
+    (e.ends_at === null || typeof e.ends_at === "string") &&
+    typeof e.place_label === "string"
+  );
+};
+
+// UX3 Add to calendar: which events this account added on this device, with the start, end and place they
+// were added with, so "Update calendar" can show after a change. Cleared at sign-out and account deletion.
+// A convenience: unreadable data reads as nothing added.
+export function createCalendarRecordStore(storage: KeyStore) {
+  let queue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
+  const run = <T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
+    const next = queue.then(work, work);
+    queue = next.catch(() => {});
+    return next;
+  };
+  const key = (scope: string) => `resbite.calendar-records.v1.${scope}`;
+  async function read(scope: string): Promise<Entry[]> {
+    const raw = await storage.getItem(key(scope));
+    let items: unknown;
+    try {
+      items = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
+    } catch {
+      return [];
+    }
+    return Array.isArray(items) ? items.filter(isEntry).slice(-CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX) : [];
+  }
+  return {
+    /** What this phone added for the event, or null when it wasn't added here. */
+    get: (scope: string, planId: string): Promise<CalendarRecord | null> =>
+      run(() => read(scope))
+        .then((entries) => {
+          const entry = entries.find((e) => e.planId === planId);
+          return entry ? { starts_at: entry.starts_at, ends_at: entry.ends_at, place_label: entry.place_label } : null;
+        })
+        .catch(() => null),
+    remember: (scope: string, planId: string, record: CalendarRecord, assertCurrent: () => void = () => {}) =>
+      run(async () => {
+        assertCurrent();
+        const current = await read(scope);
+        assertCurrent();
+        const entry: Entry = { planId, starts_at: record.starts_at, ends_at: record.ends_at, place_label: record.place_label };
+        const next = [...current.filter((e) => e.planId !== planId), entry].slice(-CALENDAR_RECORDS_MAX);
+        await storage.setItem(key(scope), JSON.stringify(next));
+      }),
+    clear: (scope: string) => run(() => storage.removeItem(key(scope))),
+  };
+}
+export type CalendarRecordStore = ReturnType<typeof createCalendarRecordStore>;
diff --git a/src/services/deviceCalendar.ts b/src/services/deviceCalendar.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2e32cdd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/deviceCalendar.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+import { Platform } from "react-native";
+import { storage } from "./supabase";
+import type { CalendarRecord, CalendarSource } from "../domain/calendarEvent";
+import { createCalendarRecordStore } from "./calendarRecordStore";
+import {
+  createCalendarService,
+  createFakeCalendarAdapter,
+  nativeCalendarAdapter,
+  type CalendarService,
+} from "./calendar";
+import { deviceScope, type ReminderScope } from "./reminders";
+
+// UX3 Add to calendar on this phone. Native opens iPhone's own form; web and preview use the fake
+// (the plan's "Device fakes" rule).
+const records = createCalendarRecordStore(storage);
+const fake = createCalendarService(createFakeCalendarAdapter(), records);
+let native: CalendarService | undefined;
+const serviceFor = (preview: boolean) =>
+  preview || Platform.OS === "web" ? fake : (native ??= createCalendarService(nativeCalendarAdapter(), records));
+
+/**
+ * "Add to calendar" (update false) or "Update calendar" (update true): opens the form filled in for the
+ * event and returns whether it was added. Returns false without opening anything when signed out.
+ */
+export async function addToCalendar(
+  scope: Pick<ReminderScope, "userId" | "preview">,
+  plan: CalendarSource & { id: string },
+  update: boolean,
+  assertCurrent?: () => void,
+): Promise<boolean> {
+  const key = deviceScope(scope);
+  if (!key) return false;
+  return serviceFor(scope.preview).add(key, plan, update, assertCurrent);
+}
+
+/** What this phone added for the event, for `needsCalendarUpdate`; null when it wasn't added here. */
+export async function calendarRecord(
+  scope: Pick<ReminderScope, "userId" | "preview">,
+  planId: string,
+): Promise<CalendarRecord | null> {
+  const key = deviceScope(scope);
+  return key ? records.get(key, planId) : null;
+}
+
+/** Sign-out and account deletion. */
+export const clearCalendarRecords = (scope: string): Promise<void> => records.clear(scope);
diff --git a/src/services/deviceMirror.ts b/src/services/deviceMirror.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e42f6f7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/deviceMirror.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+import type { CalendarEventDetails } from "../domain/calendarEvent";
+
+/**
+ * UX3 (the plan's "Device fakes" rule): on web and in preview, reminders and calendar entries go to in-memory fakes. In
+ * development builds the fakes also record what they did here, on `globalThis.__resbiteDevice`, so the
+ * browser QA scripts can check it. `permission` is the fake reminder permission; a script may set it
+ * ("denied", say) before a tap. Never present in a release build.
+ */
+export type DeviceMirror = {
+  reminders: { id: string; at: string; body: string }[];
+  calendar: CalendarEventDetails[];
+  permission: "undetermined" | "granted" | "denied";
+};
+
+declare global {
+  var __resbiteDevice: DeviceMirror | undefined;
+}
+
+export function deviceMirror(): DeviceMirror | null {
+  if (typeof __DEV__ === "undefined" || !__DEV__) return null;
+  return (globalThis.__resbiteDevice ??= { reminders: [], calendar: [], permission: "undetermined" });
+}
diff --git a/src/services/deviceReminders.ts b/src/services/deviceReminders.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d020e4b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/deviceReminders.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,144 @@
+import { Platform } from "react-native";
+import { storage, supabase } from "./supabase";
+import { withDeadline } from "./plans";
+import type { NotificationPermission } from "../domain/notifications";
+import { REMINDER_PREFIX } from "../domain/reminders";
+import { createReminderPrefsStore, type ReminderPrefs } from "./reminderPrefsStore";
+import {
+  createFakeReminderAdapter,
+  createReminderService,
+  createReminderSync,
+  deviceScope,
+  nativeReminderAdapter,
+  reminderSources,
+  reminderTapPlan,
+  type ReminderScope,
+  type ReminderService,
+} from "./reminders";
+
+export type { ReminderScope } from "./reminders";
+export type { ReminderPrefs } from "./reminderPrefsStore";
+
+// UX3 reminders on this phone. Native uses expo-notifications; web and preview use the in-memory fake
+// (the plan's "Device fakes" rule). Preview never reads the server.
+const prefs = createReminderPrefsStore(storage);
+const fake = createReminderService(createFakeReminderAdapter());
+let native: ReminderService | undefined;
+const serviceFor = (preview: boolean) =>
+  preview || Platform.OS === "web" ? fake : (native ??= createReminderService(nativeReminderAdapter()));
+
+/** The person's upcoming events: the ones they organize, and the ones they're invited to with their answer. */
+async function readSources(userId: string, now: Date) {
+  const [plans, answers] = await Promise.all([
+    withDeadline((signal) =>
+      supabase
+        .from("plans")
+        .select("id,title,starts_at,status,owner_id")
+        .eq("status", "active")
+        .gt("starts_at", now.toISOString())
+        .abortSignal(signal),
+    ),
+    withDeadline((signal) =>
+      supabase.from("attendees").select("plan_id,response").eq("user_id", userId).abortSignal(signal),
+    ),
+  ]);
+  if (plans.error) throw plans.error;
+  if (answers.error) throw answers.error;
+  return reminderSources(plans.data ?? [], answers.data ?? [], { userId, preview: false });
+}
+
+const reminders = createReminderSync({ service: serviceFor, prefs: (scope) => prefs.read(scope), readSources });
+
+/**
+ * Reconciles this phone's reminders with the person's events: when Resbite opens or comes back, and after
+ * any answer, edit, cancellation or event load. It never asks for permission. Callers may ignore failures
+ * (`void syncReminders(scope).catch(() => {})`); the next sync retries.
+ */
+export const syncReminders = (scope: ReminderScope): Promise<void> => reminders.sync(scope);
+
+/** The reminder permission on this phone (on web and in preview, the fake's). */
+export const reminderPermission = (scope: Pick<ReminderScope, "preview">): Promise<NotificationPermission> =>
+  serviceFor(scope.preview).permission();
+
+/** "Allow reminders": shows iPhone's prompt when it hasn't been answered, then schedules. Returns the result. */
+export async function askForReminders(scope: ReminderScope): Promise<NotificationPermission> {
+  const permission = await serviceFor(scope.preview).request();
+  void syncReminders(scope).catch(() => {});
+  return permission;
+}
+
+const prefsScope = (scope: ReminderScope) => {
+  const key = deviceScope(scope);
+  if (!key) throw Error("Sign in to change reminders.");
+  return key;
+};
+
+/** The person's reminder switches on this phone; the defaults when signed out. */
+export const remindersFor = (scope: ReminderScope): Promise<ReminderPrefs> => {
+  const key = deviceScope(scope);
+  return key ? prefs.read(key) : Promise.resolve({ enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+};
+
+/** The "Event reminders" switch. Re-syncs, so turning it off removes them and on sets them again. */
+export async function setRemindersEnabled(
+  scope: ReminderScope,
+  on: boolean,
+  assertCurrent?: () => void,
+): Promise<ReminderPrefs> {
+  const next = await prefs.setEnabled(prefsScope(scope), on, assertCurrent);
+  void syncReminders(scope).catch(() => {});
+  return next;
+}
+
+/** One event's "Reminders on" / "Reminders off" chip. Re-syncs. */
+export async function muteReminders(
+  scope: ReminderScope,
+  planId: string,
+  muted: boolean,
+  assertCurrent?: () => void,
+): Promise<ReminderPrefs> {
+  const next = await prefs.setMuted(prefsScope(scope), planId, muted, assertCurrent);
+  void syncReminders(scope).catch(() => {});
+  return next;
+}
+
+/** Records that the "Get reminders?" sheet was shown, so it's shown once. */
+export const markRemindersAsked = (scope: ReminderScope, assertCurrent?: () => void): Promise<ReminderPrefs> =>
+  prefs.markAsked(prefsScope(scope), assertCurrent);
+
+/** Sign-out and account deletion: removes the scope's reminders (scheduled and shown) and its switches. */
+export async function clearReminders(scope: string): Promise<void> {
+  await reminders.clear(scope);
+  await prefs.clear(scope);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Root layout: shows a reminder that arrives while Resbite is open (banner and list, no sound or badge) and
+ * reports taps on reminders, including the one that opened the app. Other notifications are left alone.
+ * Returns the cleanup. Web has no notifications.
+ */
+export function listenForReminders(open: (planId: string) => void): () => void {
+  if (Platform.OS === "web") return () => {};
+  const notifications = require("expo-notifications") as typeof import("expo-notifications");
+  notifications.setNotificationHandler({
+    handleNotification: async (notification) => {
+      const ours = notification.request.identifier.startsWith(REMINDER_PREFIX);
+      return { shouldShowBanner: ours, shouldShowList: ours, shouldPlaySound: false, shouldSetBadge: false };
+    },
+  });
+  const tapped = (response: Parameters<typeof reminderTapPlan>[0]) => {
+    const planId = reminderTapPlan(response);
+    if (planId) open(planId);
+  };
+  // Cold start: the tap that launched the app. Cleared once handled, so a remount never repeats it.
+  const launch = notifications.getLastNotificationResponse();
+  if (launch) {
+    notifications.clearLastNotificationResponse();
+    tapped(launch);
+  }
+  const subscription = notifications.addNotificationResponseReceivedListener(tapped);
+  return () => {
+    subscription.remove();
+    notifications.setNotificationHandler(null);
+  };
+}

+}
```
