       if (preview || !supported)
diff --git a/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.ts b/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..23d9ca2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/reminderPrefsStore.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+import type { KeyStore } from "./secureChunks";
+
+/** UX3 reminder switches: all reminders on or off, events muted one by one, and whether "Get reminders?" was shown. */
+export type ReminderPrefs = { enabled: boolean; muted: string[]; asked: boolean };
+/** The most muted events kept; the oldest mute drops off first. */
+export const MUTED_MAX = 200;
+const defaults = (): ReminderPrefs => ({ enabled: true, muted: [], asked: false });
+
+// UX3: the account's reminder switches, on this device only, cleared at sign-out and account deletion.
+// A convenience: unreadable data reads as the defaults (reminders on, none muted, not asked yet).
+export function createReminderPrefsStore(storage: KeyStore) {
+  let queue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
+  const run = <T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
+    const next = queue.then(work, work);
+    queue = next.catch(() => {});
+    return next;
+  };
+  const key = (scope: string) => `resbite.reminder-prefs.v1.${scope}`;
+  async function read(scope: string): Promise<ReminderPrefs> {
+    const raw = await storage.getItem(key(scope));
+    let saved: unknown;
+    try {
+      saved = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
+    } catch {
+      return defaults();
+    }
+    if (typeof saved !== "object" || saved === null) return defaults();
+    const { enabled, muted, asked } = saved as Record<string, unknown>;
+    return {
+      enabled: typeof enabled === "boolean" ? enabled : true,
+      muted: Array.isArray(muted)
+        ? [...new Set(muted.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === "string" && id !== ""))].slice(-MUTED_MAX)
+        : [],
+      asked: asked === true,
+    };
+  }
+  const change = (scope: string, edit: (prefs: ReminderPrefs) => ReminderPrefs, assertCurrent: () => void) =>
+    run(async () => {
+      assertCurrent();
+      const next = edit(await read(scope));
+      assertCurrent();
+      await storage.setItem(key(scope), JSON.stringify(next));
+      return next;
+    });
+  return {
+    read: (scope: string): Promise<ReminderPrefs> => run(() => read(scope)).catch(defaults),
+    setEnabled: (scope: string, enabled: boolean, assertCurrent: () => void = () => {}) =>
+      change(scope, (prefs) => ({ ...prefs, enabled }), assertCurrent),
+    /** Mutes or unmutes one event's reminders; the newest mute is kept last. */
+    setMuted: (scope: string, planId: string, muted: boolean, assertCurrent: () => void = () => {}) =>
+      change(
+        scope,
+        (prefs) => {
+          const others = prefs.muted.filter((id) => id !== planId);
+          return { ...prefs, muted: muted ? [...others, planId].slice(-MUTED_MAX) : others };
+        },
+        assertCurrent,
+      ),
+    markAsked: (scope: string, assertCurrent: () => void = () => {}) =>
+      change(scope, (prefs) => ({ ...prefs, asked: true }), assertCurrent),
+    clear: (scope: string) => run(() => storage.removeItem(key(scope))),
+  };
+}
+export type ReminderPrefsStore = ReturnType<typeof createReminderPrefsStore>;
diff --git a/src/services/reminders.ts b/src/services/reminders.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..cd82c2a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/services/reminders.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,243 @@
+import type { NotificationPermission } from "../domain/notifications";
+import {
+  plannedReminders,
+  REMINDER_PREFIX,
+  reminderChanges,
+  type Reminder,
+  type ReminderSource,
+  type ScheduledReminder,
+} from "../domain/reminders";
+import { permissionFrom, type NotificationPermissionResponse } from "./notifications";
+import { deviceMirror, type DeviceMirror } from "./deviceMirror";
+import type { ReminderPrefs } from "./reminderPrefsStore";
+
+// UX3 reminders (spec "Calendar and reminders", the plan's "Device fakes" rule): local notifications scheduled on this
+// phone, 1 day and 1 hour before the start. Only identifiers starting with REMINDER_PREFIX are ever touched.
+
+export type ReminderAdapter = {
+  platform: string;
+  getPermission(): Promise<NotificationPermissionResponse>;
+  /** Shows iPhone's prompt when it hasn't been answered; never called without the person asking. */
+  requestPermission(): Promise<NotificationPermissionResponse>;
+  scheduled(): Promise<ScheduledReminder[]>;
+  schedule(reminder: Reminder): Promise<void>;
+  cancel(id: string): Promise<void>;
+  /** Identifiers of notifications already delivered and still in Notification Center. */
+  delivered(): Promise<string[]>;
+  dismiss(id: string): Promise<void>;
+};
+
+/** Reminders are scheduled only with full or quiet permission. */
+export const remindersAllowed = (permission: NotificationPermission) =>
+  permission === "granted" || permission === "provisional";
+
+export function createReminderService(adapter: ReminderAdapter) {
+  return {
+    permission: async (): Promise<NotificationPermission> =>
+      permissionFrom(adapter.platform, await adapter.getPermission()),
+    request: async (): Promise<NotificationPermission> =>
+      permissionFrom(adapter.platform, await adapter.requestPermission()),
+    /** Makes the phone hold exactly `wanted`: cancels stale or changed reminders, then schedules missing ones. */
+    async sync(wanted: readonly Reminder[]): Promise<void> {
+      const { cancel, schedule } = reminderChanges(await adapter.scheduled(), wanted);
+      let failed = false;
+      // One failure doesn't stop the rest; the next sync retries whatever is still different.
+      for (const id of cancel) await adapter.cancel(id).catch(() => (failed = true));
+      for (const reminder of schedule) await adapter.schedule(reminder).catch(() => (failed = true));
+      if (failed) throw Error("Some reminders couldn’t be updated on this phone.");
+    },
+    /** Sign-out: removes every Resbite reminder, scheduled or already shown. Failures are reported. */
+    async clearAll(): Promise<void> {
+      for (const { id } of await adapter.scheduled())
+        if (id.startsWith(REMINDER_PREFIX)) await adapter.cancel(id);
+      for (const id of await adapter.delivered())
+        if (id.startsWith(REMINDER_PREFIX)) await adapter.dismiss(id);
+    },
+  };
+}
+export type ReminderService = ReturnType<typeof createReminderService>;
+
+/** When a scheduled reminder fires. iOS turns a date trigger into a time interval, so the time is kept in its data. */
+export function scheduledAt(request: { content: { data?: Record<string, unknown> | null }; trigger: unknown }): number | null {
+  const at = request.content.data?.at;
+  if (typeof at === "number" && Number.isFinite(at)) return at;
+  // Android reports a date trigger as { type: "date", value }.
+  const trigger = request.trigger as { type?: unknown; value?: unknown } | null;
+  return trigger?.type === "date" && typeof trigger.value === "number" ? trigger.value : null;
+}
+
+/** The plan a tapped notification opens: only Resbite reminders, and only with a plan id. */
+export function reminderTapPlan(
+  response: { notification: { request: { identifier: string; content: { data?: Record<string, unknown> | null } } } } | null,
+): string | null {
+  const request = response?.notification.request;
+  if (!request?.identifier.startsWith(REMINDER_PREFIX)) return null;
+  const planId = request.content.data?.planId;
+  return typeof planId === "string" && planId !== "" ? planId : null;
+}
+
+/** The real phone, through expo-notifications (already installed; local notifications need no push setup). */
+export function nativeReminderAdapter(): ReminderAdapter {
+  // Avoid evaluating unrelated legacy native modules through a namespace import.
+  const { Platform } = require("react-native") as typeof import("react-native");
+  const notifications = () => require("expo-notifications") as typeof import("expo-notifications");
+  return {
+    platform: Platform.OS,
+    getPermission: () => notifications().getPermissionsAsync(),
+    requestPermission: () =>
+      notifications().requestPermissionsAsync({ ios: { allowAlert: true, allowSound: true, allowBadge: false } }),
+    async scheduled() {
+      return (await notifications().getAllScheduledNotificationsAsync()).map((request) => ({
+        id: request.identifier,
+        at: scheduledAt(request),
+        body: request.content.body ?? "",
+      }));
+    },
+    async schedule(reminder) {
+      const native = notifications();
+      await native.scheduleNotificationAsync({
+        identifier: reminder.id,
+        // The default sound when it arrives in the background (plan decision P9); the foreground handler
+        // keeps it silent while Resbite is open (listenForReminders).
+        content: {
+          body: reminder.body,
+          sound: "default",
+          data: { planId: reminder.planId, at: reminder.at.getTime() },
+        },
+        trigger: { type: native.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.DATE, date: reminder.at },
+      });
+    },
+    cancel: (id) => notifications().cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(id),
+    async delivered() {
+      return (await notifications().getPresentedNotificationsAsync()).map((n) => n.request.identifier);
+    },
+    dismiss: (id) => notifications().dismissNotificationAsync(id),
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Web and preview: reminders kept in memory, never shown. The permission starts unanswered and the first
+ * request allows it, as a phone would. In development builds it all shows on `globalThis.__resbiteDevice`.
+ */
+export function createFakeReminderAdapter(mirror: DeviceMirror | null = deviceMirror()): ReminderAdapter {
+  const reminders = new Map<string, Reminder>();
+  // In development the mirror holds the permission, so a QA script can change it.
+  let permission: DeviceMirror["permission"] = "undetermined";
+  const current = () => mirror?.permission ?? permission;
+  const publish = () => {
+    if (!mirror) return;
+    const list = [...reminders.values()]
+      .sort((a, b) => a.at.getTime() - b.at.getTime())
+      .map((r) => ({ id: r.id, at: r.at.toISOString(), body: r.body }));
+    mirror.reminders.splice(0, mirror.reminders.length, ...list);
+  };
+  return {
+    platform: "fake",
+    getPermission: async () => ({ status: current() }),
+    async requestPermission() {
+      if (current() === "undetermined") {
+        permission = "granted";
+        if (mirror) mirror.permission = "granted";
+      }
+      return { status: current() };
+    },
+    scheduled: async () => [...reminders.values()].map((r) => ({ id: r.id, at: r.at.getTime(), body: r.body })),
+    async schedule(reminder) {
+      reminders.set(reminder.id, reminder);
+      publish();
+    },
+    async cancel(id) {
+      reminders.delete(id);
+      publish();
+    },
+    delivered: async () => [],
+    dismiss: async () => {},
+  };
+}
+
+/** A plan as the reminder sync reads it: My resbites' rows, or preview's plans on the device. */
+export type ReminderPlanRow = { id: string; title: string; starts_at: string; status: string; owner_id?: string };
+
+/** What reminders are planned from: the person's plans, with their own answer on each. */
+export function reminderSources(
+  plans: readonly ReminderPlanRow[],
+  answers: readonly { plan_id: string; response: string }[],
+  who: { userId: string | null; preview: boolean },
+): ReminderSource[] {
+  const responses = new Map(answers.map((a) => [a.plan_id, a.response]));
+  return plans.map((plan) => ({
+    id: plan.id,
+    title: plan.title,
+    starts_at: plan.starts_at,
+    status: plan.status,
+    // Preview has no accounts: everything on the device is the person's own.
+    owner: who.preview || (who.userId !== null && plan.owner_id === who.userId),
+    response: responses.get(plan.id) ?? null,
+  }));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Who the reminders are for. Preview passes its plans (they live on the device); a call without them reuses
+ * the last ones passed. `userId: null` outside preview means signed out: the phone keeps no reminders.
+ */
+export type ReminderScope = { userId: string | null; preview: boolean; localPlans?: readonly ReminderPlanRow[] };
+/** The per-account key for the stores on this device: "preview", the account id, or null when signed out. */
+export const deviceScope = (scope: { userId: string | null; preview: boolean }) =>
+  scope.preview ? "preview" : scope.userId;
+
+type SyncDependencies = {
+  service: (preview: boolean) => ReminderService;
+  prefs: (scope: string) => Promise<ReminderPrefs>;
+  /** The signed-in person's upcoming plans and their own answers. A failure leaves the phone unchanged. */
+  readSources: (userId: string, now: Date) => Promise<ReminderSource[]>;
+  now?: () => Date;
+};
+
+/**
+ * Reconciles the phone's reminders with the person's events. Calls run one at a time and a newer call
+ * supersedes an older one that hasn't reached the phone yet. It never asks for permission: without it, or
+ * with reminders switched off, the phone keeps none.
+ */
+export function createReminderSync(deps: SyncDependencies) {
+  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date());
+  let generation = 0;
+  let previewPlans: readonly ReminderPlanRow[] = [];
+  let queue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
+  const run = <T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
+    const next = queue.then(work, work);
+    queue = next.catch(() => {});
+    return next;
+  };
+  async function wanted(scope: ReminderScope, service: ReminderService, plans: readonly ReminderPlanRow[]) {
+    const key = deviceScope(scope);
+    if (!key || !remindersAllowed(await service.permission())) return [];
+    const prefs = await deps.prefs(key);
+    if (!prefs.enabled) return [];
+    const at = now();
+    const sources =
+      scope.preview || !scope.userId
+        ? reminderSources(plans, [], scope)
+        : await deps.readSources(scope.userId, at);
+    return plannedReminders(sources, { now: at, enabled: prefs.enabled, muted: new Set(prefs.muted) });
+  }
+  return {
+    sync(scope: ReminderScope): Promise<void> {
+      if (scope.preview && scope.localPlans) previewPlans = scope.localPlans;
+      const plans = previewPlans;
+      const mine = ++generation;
+      return run(async () => {
+        if (mine !== generation) return;
+        const service = deps.service(scope.preview);
+        const reminders = await wanted(scope, service, plans);
+        if (mine !== generation) return;
+        await service.sync(reminders);
+      });
+    },
+    /** Sign-out: removes every reminder for that scope's phone (or preview's fake), after any sync in flight. */
+    clear(scope: string): Promise<void> {
+      ++generation;
+      if (scope === "preview") previewPlans = [];
+      return run(() => deps.service(scope === "preview").clearAll());
+    },
+  };
+}

diff --git a/src/state/useDeviceReminders.ts b/src/state/useDeviceReminders.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b94609a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/state/useDeviceReminders.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
+import { AppState } from "react-native";
+import { router, useRootNavigationState } from "expo-router";
+import { useApp } from "./AppState";
+import { useAccount } from "./AccountState";
+import { listenForReminders, syncReminders, type ReminderScope } from "../services/deviceReminders";
+
+/**
+ * UX3 reminders for the whole app, from the root layout. Keeps this phone's reminders in step with the
+ * session (preview on its fake), re-syncs when Resbite comes back to the foreground, and opens the event
+ * when a reminder is tapped, after the usual sign-in and account checks. `ready` is false while the root
+ * layout holds the navigator back (restoring the session or the first account check).
+ */
+export function useDeviceReminders(ready: boolean) {
+  const { session, preview, restoring, signingOut, localPlans } = useApp();
+  const account = useAccount();
+  const userId = session?.user.id ?? null;
+  const accountReady = account.status === "approved" && account.reviewed;
+  const previewPlans = preview ? localPlans : null;
+  const scope = useRef<ReminderScope | null>(null);
+  useEffect(() => {
+    // Sign-out clears reminders itself; never sync over it.
+    if (restoring || signingOut) {
+      scope.current = null;
+      return;
+    }
+    // Signed in: once the account is checked. Signed out: the phone keeps none, even when a session
+    // ended without signing out.
+    const next: ReminderScope | null = previewPlans
+      ? { userId, preview: true, localPlans: previewPlans }
+      : userId
+        ? accountReady
+          ? { userId, preview: false }
+          : null
+        : { userId: null, preview: false };
+    scope.current = next;
+    if (next) void syncReminders(next).catch(() => {});
+  }, [restoring, signingOut, userId, accountReady, previewPlans]);
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let previous = AppState.currentState;
+    const subscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
+      if (state === "active" && previous !== "active" && scope.current)
+        void syncReminders(scope.current).catch(() => {});
+      previous = state;
+    });
+    return () => subscription.remove();
+  }, []);
+
+  const [tapped, setTapped] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  useEffect(() => listenForReminders(setTapped), []);
+  const navigation = useRootNavigationState();
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!tapped || restoring) return;
+    // A reminder belongs to a signed-in account; preview never shows one.
+    if (!session || preview) {
+      setTapped(null);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (!accountReady || !ready || !navigation?.key) return;
+    setTapped(null);
+    router.push({ pathname: "/event", params: { plan: tapped } });
+  }, [tapped, restoring, session, preview, accountReady, ready, navigation?.key]);
+}
```

  2. `npx expo config --type introspect | grep -E "NSCalendars|NSReminders|applinks"`. Expected:

+test("UX3 time ranges: cards and Review read the end when there is one; labels without an end are unchanged", () =>
+  inZone("Europe/London", () => {
+    const now = new Date("2026-10-07T12:00:00+01:00");

+    const end = new Date(2026, 9, 10, 12, 0).toISOString();
+    const late = new Date(2026, 9, 11, 2, 0).toISOString();
+    assert.equal(spokenWhen(start, null, now), "Saturday 10 October at 10:00");
+    assert.equal(spokenWhen(start, end, now), "Saturday 10 October, from 10:00 to 12:00");
+    assert.equal(spokenWhen(start, late, now), "Saturday 10 October, from 10:00 to 02:00 on Sunday 11 October");

+
+/** The end card's "Remove" action: a 44 pt text button that reads "Remove end time". */
+function RemoveEnd({ onPress, disabled }: { onPress: () => void; disabled: boolean }) {

+            editable={!disabled}
+            onFocus={() => setFocused(true)}
+            onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
+            style={[styles.input, styles.noOutline]}
+          />
+        </View>

+      {below ? <View style={styles.below}>{accessory}</View> : null}

+/**
+ * How a card's date and time read to VoiceOver: "Saturday 10 October at 10:00", or with an end (UX3)

+ */
+export function spokenWhen(startIso: string | null, endIso: string | null | undefined, now: Date): string {
+  const date = parse(startIso);
+  if (!date) return "";
+  const range = spokenRange(startIso, endIso, now);
+  return range ? `${spokenDate(date, now)}, ${range}` : spokenDateTime(date, now);

  - verify-steps (11 PASS lines): "PASS: the optional end time: added an hour after the start, keeps its length, checked on When, may end the next day, Remove clears it, cards show the range." and "PASS: Larger Text at 320 px on every step: no sideways overflow, inline pill."
  - verify-planning (18 PASS lines): "PASS: the end time is sent as p_ends (none after Remove) and shown as a range; Meeting details load in the editor."

### Task 8: The event page: Note to guests, Meeting details, calendar, reminders and "Can’t go"

**Files:**
- Create: `mobile/src/design/EventNotes.tsx`: `GuestNoteCard`, `GuestNoteSheet` and `MeetingDetails`
- Create: `mobile/src/design/EventDeviceChips.tsx`: `EventDeviceChips` and `ReminderSheet`
- Create: `mobile/src/state/useEventDevice.ts`: the calendar and reminder state for one event
- Create: `mobile/src/domain/eventDevice.ts` (+ `eventDevice.test.ts`): chip wording and when to ask
- Modify: `mobile/app/event.tsx`, `mobile/src/state/useEvent.ts` (syncs reminders after loads, answers and cancellations)
- Modify: `mobile/src/domain/rsvp.ts` (+ `rsvp.test.ts`) and `mobile/src/domain/eventView.ts` (+ `eventView.test.ts`): the "Can’t go" wording
- Modify: `mobile/src/design/kit.tsx` (one comment)
- Modify: `mobile/scripts/qa-helpers.mjs` (`quietReminders`), `mobile/scripts/verify-event.mjs`, `mobile/scripts/verify-rsvp.mjs`, and `quietReminders` in `verify-planning`, `verify-steps`, `verify-catalogue`, `verify-covers`, `verify-people` and `review-ui`
- Modify: `mobile/package.json` (the `test` chain)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 4: `meetingDetailsView`, `MEETING_DETAILS_LOCKED`, `GUEST_NOTE_MAX`, `guestNoteError`, `guestNoteTime`, `canPostGuestNote`, `canAddToCalendar`, `needsCalendarUpdate`.
  - Task 5: `setGuestNote`, `Plan.guest_note`, `guest_note_at`, `has_meeting_details`.
  - Task 6: `addToCalendar`, `calendarRecord`, `syncReminders`, `reminderPermission`, `askForReminders`, `remindersFor`, `muteReminders`, `markRemindersAsked`, `remindersAllowed`.
  - Task 7: `spokenWhen`, `CoverCard` `endsAt`.
- Produces:
  - `cantGoResponse(current)` replaces `cantMakeItResponse` in `src/domain/rsvp.ts`. The answer reads "Can’t go", or "Can’t go now" after Going (UX3-D7).
  - `src/domain/eventDevice.ts`:
    - `reminderChip({ permission, allowed, enabled, muted }): ReminderChip`;
    - `shouldAskForReminders({ permission, asked, enabled })`;
    - `GET_REMINDERS_LINE`;
    - `CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED` ("We couldn’t open your calendar. Add to calendar needs iOS 17 or later.");
    - `REMINDERS_CHANGE_FAILED` ("Couldn’t change reminders on this phone. Try again.").
  - `useEventDevice(plan, { owner, response }): EventDevice`, with `{ shown, calendarUpdate, reminders, busy, error, asking, addToCalendar, pressReminders, allowReminders, notNow }`.
  - On the event page:
    - The Note to guests card sits at the top ("Note to guests · 9:41"). The organizer posts it with "Post a note to guests", while `canPostGuestNote` holds, through a sheet with "Post" and "Remove note".
    - The Meeting details card ("Meeting details · <place>", "Meeting details · only Going & Maybe see this" for the organizer) shows to insiders. Everyone else sees the locked line when details exist.
    - The "Calendar and reminders" chips: "Add to calendar" (or "Update calendar"), and "Reminders on, 1 day and 1 hour before", "Reminders on" or "Reminders off".
    - The "Get reminders?" sheet ("Allow reminders" / "Not now") shows once per phone: the first time the chips appear (on the organizer's own event, or after a guest answers Going or Maybe) while iPhone's permission hasn't been asked. Showing it counts as asked, whatever the answer.
  - `quietReminders(target)` in `qa-helpers.mjs` starts a browser page or context with the fake reminder permission set to `"denied"`, so older checks never meet the "Get reminders?" sheet.

+/**
+ * UX3: the event page asks "Get reminders?" the first time an organizer or a Going or Maybe guest opens one
+ * while the phone's permission was never answered. Scripts that aren't about reminders answer it up front on
+ * the development fake (`__resbiteDevice`): "denied" schedules nothing, reads nothing and never asks.
+ * `target` is a page or a browser context.
+ */
+export async function quietReminders(target) {
+  await target.addInitScript(() => {
+    globalThis.__resbiteDevice ??= { reminders: [], calendar: [], permission: "denied" };
+  });
+}

diff --git a/src/domain/eventDevice.test.ts b/src/domain/eventDevice.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..70fbbc1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/domain/eventDevice.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED, GET_REMINDERS_LINE, reminderChip, shouldAskForReminders } from "./eventDevice.ts";
+
+const chip = (permission: Parameters<typeof reminderChip>[0]["permission"], enabled = true, muted = false) =>
+  reminderChip({ permission, allowed: permission === "granted" || permission === "provisional", enabled, muted });
+
+test("the reminders chip is on only with permission, the switch on and the event not muted", () => {
+  assert.deepEqual(chip("granted"), {
+    on: true,
+    text: "Reminders on",
+    label: "Reminders on, 1 day and 1 hour before",
+    hint: "Turns reminders off for this resbite",
+    action: "mute",
+  });
+  assert.equal(chip("provisional").on, true);
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    [chip("granted", true, true), chip("undetermined"), chip("denied"), chip("unknown"), chip("ephemeral")].map((c) => [
+      c.on,
+      c.text,
+      c.label,
+      c.action,
+    ]),
+    [
+      [false, "Reminders off", "Reminders off", "unmute"],
+      [false, "Reminders off", "Reminders off", "ask"],
+      [false, "Reminders off", "Reminders off", "settings"],
+      [false, "Reminders off", "Reminders off", "settings"],
+      [false, "Reminders off", "Reminders off", "settings"],
+    ],
+  );
+});
+
+test("switched off on Notifications, the chip reads Reminders off and opens Notifications, whatever the permission", () => {
+  for (const permission of ["granted", "undetermined", "denied"] as const) {
+    const off = chip(permission, false);
+    assert.equal(off.on, false);
+    assert.equal(off.text, "Reminders off");
+    assert.equal(off.action, "notifications");
+  }
+});
+
+test("Get reminders? asks once, only before iPhone's prompt and only with reminders switched on", () => {
+  assert.equal(shouldAskForReminders({ permission: "undetermined", asked: false, enabled: true }), true);
+  assert.equal(shouldAskForReminders({ permission: "undetermined", asked: true, enabled: true }), false);
+  assert.equal(shouldAskForReminders({ permission: "undetermined", asked: false, enabled: false }), false);
+  for (const permission of ["granted", "provisional", "denied", "preview", "unsupported"] as const)
+    assert.equal(shouldAskForReminders({ permission, asked: false, enabled: true }), false, permission);
+});
+
+test("the sheet and the calendar failure keep their wording", () => {
+  assert.equal(
+    GET_REMINDERS_LINE,
+    "Resbite can remind you 1 day and 1 hour before your resbites. Reminders are set on this phone, and only show the name and time.",
+  );
+  assert.equal(CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED, "We couldn’t open your calendar. Add to calendar needs iOS 17 or later.");
+});

diff --git a/src/design/EventDeviceChips.tsx b/src/design/EventDeviceChips.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ba5d915
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/design/EventDeviceChips.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+import React from "react";
+import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
+import { Bell, BellOff, CalendarPlus, CalendarSync } from "lucide-react-native";
+import { Button, Copy } from "./ui";
+import { Chip, InfoSheet } from "./kit";
+import { GET_REMINDERS_LINE } from "../domain/eventDevice";
+import type { EventDevice } from "../state/useEventDevice";
+
+// UX3 Add to calendar and reminders on the event page (spec "Calendar and reminders"; mockup "Calendar and
+// reminders"). The chips sit in the event page's chip row, before Open in Maps.
+
+/** "Add to calendar" (or "Update calendar") and "Reminders on" / "Reminders off". */
+export function EventDeviceChips({ device }: { device: EventDevice }) {
+  if (!device.shown) return null;
+  const reminders = device.reminders;
+  return (
+    <>
+      <Chip
+        icon={device.calendarUpdate ? CalendarSync : CalendarPlus}
+        tone="aqua"
+        text={device.calendarUpdate ? "Update calendar" : "Add to calendar"}
+        hint={device.calendarUpdate ? "Opens your calendar with the new time and place" : "Opens your calendar"}
+        disabled={device.busy}
+        onPress={device.addToCalendar}
+      />
+      {reminders && (
+        <Chip
+          icon={reminders.on ? Bell : BellOff}
+          text={reminders.text}
+          label={reminders.label}
+          hint={reminders.hint}
+          disabled={device.busy}
+          onPress={device.pressReminders}
+        />
+      )}
+    </>
+  );
+}
+
+/** "Get reminders?": shown once, before iPhone's own prompt. Either answer counts as asked. */
+export function ReminderSheet({ device }: { device: EventDevice }) {
+  return (
+    <InfoSheet visible={device.asking} title="Get reminders?" onClose={device.notNow}>
+      <Copy>{GET_REMINDERS_LINE}</Copy>
+      <View style={styles.actions}>
+        <Button title="Allow reminders" icon={false} onPress={device.allowReminders} />
+        <Button title="Not now" secondary icon={false} onPress={device.notNow} />
+      </View>
+    </InfoSheet>
+  );
+}
+
+const styles = StyleSheet.create({
+  actions: { gap: 10 },
+});
diff --git a/src/design/EventNotes.tsx b/src/design/EventNotes.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a63061b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/design/EventNotes.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,186 @@
+import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
+import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
+import { Lock, MapPin, Megaphone, Pencil, Trash2 } from "lucide-react-native";
+import { Button, Copy, ErrorNote, TextAction } from "./ui";
+import { FieldCard, IconButton, InfoSheet } from "./kit";
+import { categoryPalette, colors as c, fonts, r, space } from "./tokens";
+import { guestNoteError, guestNoteTime, MEETING_DETAILS_LOCKED, type MeetingDetailsView } from "../domain/eventDetails";
+
+// UX3 event page notes (spec "Event details", UX3-D5; mockup "Event details"): the pinned Note to guests,
+// its sheet, and Meeting details (shown, or a locked line).
+
+const NOTE = categoryPalette.uplifting;
+const ROSE = "#963F58";
+
+/**
+ * The pinned Note to guests: yellow, with the time it was posted. The organizer's ✎ reopens the sheet; the
+ * note itself reads as one element ("Note to guests, 9:41, …").
+ */
+export function GuestNoteCard({
+  text,
+  postedAt,
+  onEdit,
+}: {
+  text: string;
+  postedAt: string | null | undefined;
+  /** The organizer, while the note can still change. */
+  onEdit?: () => void;
+}) {
+  const time = guestNoteTime(postedAt, new Date());
+  const caption = time ? `Note to guests · ${time}` : "Note to guests";
+  return (
+    <View style={styles.note}>
+      <View
+        accessible
+        accessibilityLabel={`${time ? `Note to guests, ${time}` : "Note to guests"}: ${text}`}
+        style={styles.noteBody}
+      >
+        <View aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
+          <Megaphone size={22} strokeWidth={2} color={NOTE.ink} />
+        </View>
+        <View style={styles.noteText}>
+          <Copy style={[styles.caption, { color: NOTE.ink }]}>{caption}</Copy>
+          <Copy>{text}</Copy>
+        </View>
+      </View>
+      {onEdit && <IconButton icon={Pencil} label="Edit note to guests" tone="plain" onPress={onEdit} />}
+    </View>
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Posts, changes or removes the Note to guests (up to 280 characters). `onSave` throws with the message to
+ * show; an empty note removes it. The sheet closes once the save is confirmed.
+ */
+export function GuestNoteSheet({
+  visible,
+  current,
+  onSave,
+  onClose,
+}: {
+  visible: boolean;
+  /** The posted note, or "" when there is none. */
+  current: string;
+  onSave: (text: string) => Promise<void>;
+  onClose: () => void;
+}) {
+  const [text, setText] = useState(current);
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  // Each opening starts from the posted note.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!visible) return;
+    setText(current);
+    setError(null);
+  }, [visible, current]);
+  const editing = current.trim() !== "";
+  async function save(next: string) {
+    const invalid = guestNoteError(next);
+    if (invalid) {
+      setError(invalid);
+      return;
+    }
+    setBusy(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      await onSave(next);
+      onClose();
+    } catch (e) {
+      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "We couldn’t save the note. Check your connection and try again.");
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
+  }
+  return (
+    <InfoSheet visible={visible} title="Note to guests" onClose={() => (busy ? undefined : onClose())}>
+      <FieldCard
+        label="Note to guests"
+        icon={Megaphone}
+        placeholder="Running late, bring a blanket… (optional)"
+        value={text}
+        onChangeText={setText}
+        multiline
+        minHeight={110}
+        editable={!busy}
+      />
+      <ErrorNote message={error} />
+      <Button
+        title={editing ? "Save" : "Post"}
+        icon={false}
+        loading={busy}
+        disabled={busy || (!editing && text.trim() === "")}
+        onPress={() => void save(text)}
+      />
+      {editing && (
+        <TextAction icon={Trash2} destructive title="Remove note" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void save("")} />
+      )}
+    </InfoSheet>
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Meeting details (UX3-D6): the organizer and Going or Maybe guests see the card; anyone else sees a locked
+ * line when there are some, and nothing otherwise.
+ */
+export function MeetingDetails({ view, owner, place }: { view: MeetingDetailsView; owner: boolean; place: string }) {
+  if (view.kind === "none") return null;
+  if (view.kind === "locked")
+    return (
+      <View style={styles.locked}>
+        <View aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
+          <Lock size={20} strokeWidth={2} color={c.muted} />
+        </View>
+        <Copy style={styles.lockedText}>{MEETING_DETAILS_LOCKED}</Copy>
+      </View>
+    );
+  const caption = owner ? "Meeting details · only Going & Maybe see this" : `Meeting details · ${place.trim()}`;
+  return (
+    <View style={styles.meeting}>
+      <View aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
+        <MapPin size={22} strokeWidth={2} color={ROSE} />
+      </View>
+      <View style={styles.noteText}>
+        <Copy style={styles.caption}>{caption}</Copy>
+        <Copy>{view.text}</Copy>
+      </View>
+    </View>
+  );
+}
+
+const styles = StyleSheet.create({
+  note: {
+    flexDirection: "row",
+    alignItems: "flex-start",
+    gap: space.s,
+    paddingVertical: space.l,
+    paddingLeft: 18,
+    paddingRight: space.s,
+    borderRadius: r.tile,
+    backgroundColor: NOTE.tint,
+  },
+  noteBody: { flex: 1, flexDirection: "row", gap: space.m, paddingRight: 10 },
+  noteText: { flex: 1, gap: 3 },
+  // Small capitals, as in the mockup; the words stay in sentence case for VoiceOver.
+  caption: { fontFamily: fonts.bold, fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: 0.4, textTransform: "uppercase", color: c.muted },
+  meeting: {
+    flexDirection: "row",
+    gap: space.m,
+    paddingVertical: space.l,
+    paddingHorizontal: 18,
+    borderRadius: 20,
+    backgroundColor: c.paper,
+    borderWidth: 1,
+    borderColor: "#EFEAF0",
+  },
+  locked: {
+    flexDirection: "row",
+    alignItems: "center",
+    gap: space.m,
+    minHeight: 44,
+    paddingVertical: 14,
+    paddingHorizontal: space.l,
+    borderRadius: 20,
+    backgroundColor: c.surface,
+  },
+  lockedText: { flex: 1, fontSize: 14, color: "#5D5866" },
+});

 export function Segmented<K extends string>({
   label,
   options,
diff --git a/src/domain/eventDevice.ts b/src/domain/eventDevice.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e0e5a67
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/domain/eventDevice.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,68 @@
+import type { NotificationPermission } from "./notifications";
+
+// UX3 event page: the reminders chip and the "Get reminders?" sheet (spec "Calendar and reminders", UX3-D4).
+// The calendar chip's wording comes from `needsCalendarUpdate` (calendarEvent.ts).
+
+/** What a tap on the reminders chip does. */
+export type ReminderChipAction = "mute" | "unmute" | "ask" | "settings" | "notifications";
+export type ReminderChip = { on: boolean; text: string; label: string; hint: string; action: ReminderChipAction };
+
+const off = (action: ReminderChipAction, hint: string): ReminderChip => ({
+  on: false,
+  text: "Reminders off",
+  label: "Reminders off",
+  hint,
+  action,
+});
+
+/**
+ * The event's "Reminders on" / "Reminders off" chip. It is on only with the permission (`allowed`), the
+ * Notifications switch on and this event not muted. A tap mutes or unmutes this event on this phone. When
+ * reminders are switched off on Notifications it opens Notifications; when iPhone was never asked ("Not
+ * now"), it asks; when the permission is off, it opens Settings.
+ */
+export function reminderChip(state: {
+  permission: NotificationPermission;
+  /** Full or quiet permission (`remindersAllowed`). */
+  allowed: boolean;
+  /** The Notifications screen's "Event reminders" switch. */
+  enabled: boolean;
+  /** This event's reminders are switched off on this phone. */
+  muted: boolean;
+}): ReminderChip {
+  if (!state.enabled) return off("notifications", "Opens Notifications, where reminders are switched off");
+  if (state.allowed)
+    return state.muted
+      ? off("unmute", "Turns reminders on for this resbite")
+      : {
+          on: true,
+          text: "Reminders on",
+          label: "Reminders on, 1 day and 1 hour before",
+          hint: "Turns reminders off for this resbite",
+          action: "mute",
+        };
+  if (state.permission === "undetermined") return off("ask", "Asks to allow reminders");
+  return off("settings", "Opens Settings to allow notifications");
+}
+
+/**
+ * "Get reminders?" is shown once, before iPhone's own prompt: only while the permission was never asked
+ * for, the sheet hasn't been shown on this phone and reminders aren't switched off.
+ */
+export function shouldAskForReminders(state: {
+  permission: NotificationPermission;
+  asked: boolean;
+  enabled: boolean;
+}): boolean {
+  return state.permission === "undetermined" && !state.asked && state.enabled;
+}
+
+export const GET_REMINDERS_LINE =
+  "Resbite can remind you 1 day and 1 hour before your resbites. Reminders are set on this phone, and only show the name and time.";
+
+/**
+ * Controller ruling P10: iPhone's add-event form opens without a permission only on iOS 17 or later; older
+ * versions (and a form that is already open) make it fail. Resbite never asks for calendar access.
+ */
+export const CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED = "We couldn’t open your calendar. Add to calendar needs iOS 17 or later.";
+export const REMINDERS_CHANGE_FAILED = "Couldn’t change reminders on this phone. Try again.";

   if (response === "accepted") return "Going";
   if (response === "maybe") return "Maybe";
-  if (response === "declined") return "Can’t make it";

@@ -66,17 +66,17 @@ export type GuestGroup = { title: string; guests: { userId: string; name: string
 const GUEST_GROUPS: readonly { title: string; responses: readonly string[] }[] = [

-  { title: "Can’t make it", responses: ["declined", "withdrawn"] },

 ];
 
-/** The guest sheet, grouped by answer; "Can’t make it now" marks someone who backed out after Going. */
+/** The guest sheet, grouped by answer; "Can’t go now" marks someone who backed out after Going. */
 export function guestGroups(guests: readonly GuestRow[]): GuestGroup[] {
   return GUEST_GROUPS.map(({ title, responses }) => ({
     title,
     guests: guests
       .filter((g) => responses.includes(g.response))
-      .map((g) => ({ userId: g.userId, name: g.name, note: g.response === "withdrawn" ? "Can’t make it now" : null })),
+      .map((g) => ({ userId: g.userId, name: g.name, note: g.response === "withdrawn" ? "Can’t go now" : null })),
   })).filter((group) => group.guests.length > 0);
 }

@@ -38,19 +38,19 @@ export function reconcileRsvp(
 
 export type AnswerKey = "going" | "maybe" | "cant";
 
-/** The answer control's selected option for a stored response (UX2: Going / Maybe / Can’t make it). */
+/** The answer control's selected option for a stored response (UX2: Going / Maybe; UX3-D7: Can’t go). */
 export function answerKey(response: string): AnswerKey | null {
   if (response === "accepted") return "going";
   if (response === "maybe") return "maybe";
   return response === "declined" || response === "withdrawn" ? "cant" : null;
 }
 
-/** Can’t make it withdraws after Going ("Can’t make it now"); before Going, or after Maybe, it declines. */
-export function cantMakeItResponse(current: string): Response {
+/** Can’t go withdraws after Going ("Can’t go now"); before Going, or after Maybe, it declines. */
+export function cantGoResponse(current: string): Response {
   return current === "accepted" || current === "withdrawn" ? "withdrawn" : "declined";
 }
 
 /** The response a tap on the answer control saves. */
 export function responseFor(key: AnswerKey, current: string): Response {
-  return key === "going" ? "accepted" : key === "maybe" ? "maybe" : cantMakeItResponse(current);
+  return key === "going" ? "accepted" : key === "maybe" ? "maybe" : cantGoResponse(current);
 }

diff --git a/src/state/useEventDevice.ts b/src/state/useEventDevice.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b2e008c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/state/useEventDevice.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,182 @@
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
+import { AppState } from "react-native";
+import { router, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
+import { useApp, type LocalPlan } from "./AppState";
+import { canAddToCalendar, needsCalendarUpdate } from "../domain/calendarEvent";
+import {
+  CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED,
+  REMINDERS_CHANGE_FAILED,
+  reminderChip,
+  shouldAskForReminders,
+  type ReminderChip,
+} from "../domain/eventDevice";
+import type { NotificationPermission } from "../domain/notifications";
+import { addToCalendar, calendarRecord } from "../services/deviceCalendar";
+import {
+  askForReminders,
+  markRemindersAsked,
+  muteReminders,
+  reminderPermission,
+  remindersFor,
+  type ReminderPrefs,
+} from "../services/deviceReminders";
+import { remindersAllowed } from "../services/reminders";
+import { openNotificationSettings } from "../services/notifications";
+
+export type EventDevice = {
+  /** The calendar and reminder chips show for the organizer and Going or Maybe guests, on upcoming active events. */
+  shown: boolean;
+  /** "Update calendar" once the time or place changed after it was added on this phone. */
+  calendarUpdate: boolean;
+  /** Null until this phone's reminder state has been read. */
+  reminders: ReminderChip | null;
+  busy: boolean;
+  error: string | null;
+  /** The "Get reminders?" sheet. */
+  asking: boolean;
+  addToCalendar: () => void;
+  pressReminders: () => void;
+  allowReminders: () => void;
+  notNow: () => void;
+};
+
+/**
+ * UX3 Add to calendar and reminders on the event page. Reads what this phone added and its reminder state
+ * whenever the page gains focus or Resbite returns to the foreground (Settings may have changed the
+ * permission). The first time an organizer or a Going or Maybe guest is here with iPhone's permission
+ * never asked for, it shows "Get reminders?" once; answering Going or Maybe makes the guest one, so the sheet
+ * follows a confirmed answer and never stands in its way.
+ */
+export function useEventDevice(plan: LocalPlan | null, view: { owner: boolean; response: string | null }): EventDevice {
+  const { session, preview, captureSession } = useApp();
+  const userId = session?.user.id ?? null;
+  const scope = { userId, preview };
+  const shown = plan !== null && canAddToCalendar(plan, view, Date.now());
+  const [state, setState] = useState<{
+    permission: NotificationPermission;
+    prefs: ReminderPrefs;
+    update: boolean;
+  } | null>(null);
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
+  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);
+  const working = useRef(false);
+  const revision = useRef(0);
+  const askedHere = useRef(false);
+  const identity = `${userId}:${preview}:${plan?.id}`;
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setState(null);
+    setError(null);
+    setAsking(false);
+    askedHere.current = false;
+  }, [identity]);
+  const latest = useRef({ plan, shown, scope });
+  latest.current = { plan, shown, scope };
+
+  const read = useCallback(async () => {
+    const { plan: current, shown: visible, scope: who } = latest.current;
+    if (!current || !visible) return;
+    const request = ++revision.current;
+    try {
+      const [permission, prefs, record] = await Promise.all([
+        reminderPermission(who),
+        remindersFor(who),
+        calendarRecord(who, current.id),
+      ]);
+      if (request !== revision.current) return;
+      setState({ permission, prefs, update: needsCalendarUpdate(record, current) });
+      if (!askedHere.current && shouldAskForReminders({ permission, asked: prefs.asked, enabled: prefs.enabled })) {
+        askedHere.current = true;
+        setAsking(true);
+        // Shown counts as asked, whatever the answer.
+        void markRemindersAsked(who, captureSession()).catch(() => {});
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // The chips wait for the next read; nothing here blocks the page.
+    }
+  }, [captureSession]);
+  // Re-read when the event's time or place changes, or when the person becomes a guest who's going.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    void read();
+  }, [read, identity, shown, plan?.starts_at, plan?.ends_at, plan?.place_label]);
+  useFocusEffect(
+    useCallback(() => {
+      void read();
+      const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (next) => {
+        if (next === "active") void read();
+      });
+      return () => sub.remove();
+    }, [read]),
+  );
+
+  /** One device action at a time; the chips re-read afterwards. */
+  async function run(work: () => Promise<void>, failure: string) {
+    if (working.current) return;
+    working.current = true;
+    setBusy(true);
+    setError(null);
+    try {
+      await work();
+    } catch {
+      setError(failure);
+    } finally {
+      working.current = false;
+      setBusy(false);
+      void read();
+    }
+  }
+  const chip = state
+    ? reminderChip({
+        permission: state.permission,
+        allowed: remindersAllowed(state.permission),
+        enabled: state.prefs.enabled,
+        muted: plan !== null && state.prefs.muted.includes(plan.id),
+      })
+    : null;
+
+  return {
+    shown,
+    calendarUpdate: state?.update ?? false,
+    reminders: chip,
+    busy,
+    error,
+    asking,
+    addToCalendar: () => {
+      const current = plan;
+      if (!current) return;
+      // Never asks for calendar access: iPhone's own form saves the event (plan decision P1).
+      void run(async () => {
+        await addToCalendar(scope, current, state?.update ?? false, captureSession());
+      }, CALENDAR_OPEN_FAILED);
+    },
+    pressReminders: () => {
+      const current = plan;
+      if (!current || !chip) return;
+      if (chip.action === "notifications") {
+        router.push("/notifications");
+        return;
+      }
+      if (chip.action === "settings") {
+        void run(() => openNotificationSettings(preview), "Couldn’t open device settings. Please open Settings on your phone.");
+        return;
+      }
+      void run(async () => {
+        if (chip.action === "ask") {
+          const permission = await askForReminders(scope);
+          // Turning reminders on here also turns them back on for this event.
+          if (remindersAllowed(permission) && state?.prefs.muted.includes(current.id))
+            await muteReminders(scope, current.id, false, captureSession());
+          return;
+        }
+        await muteReminders(scope, current.id, chip.action === "mute", captureSession());
+      }, REMINDERS_CHANGE_FAILED);
+    },
+    allowReminders: () => {
+      setAsking(false);
+      void run(async () => {
+        await askForReminders(scope);
+      }, REMINDERS_CHANGE_FAILED);
+    },
+    notNow: () => setAsking(false),
+  };
+}
```

@@ -826,6 +827,55 @@ export function Row({

+/** A Row with a real switch (UX3 "Event reminders"): the switch carries the title as its label. */
+export function SwitchRow({
+  title,
+  icon: Icon,
+  tint = c.aquaSoft,
+  ink = c.aquaDark,
+  value,
+  onValueChange,
+  disabled = false,
+}: {
+  title: string;
+  icon: LucideIcon;
+  tint?: string;
+  ink?: string;
+  value: boolean;
+  onValueChange: (next: boolean) => void;
+  disabled?: boolean;
+}) {
+  // The whole row (at least 60 pt) is the switch, so the target meets 44 pt; the drawn switch only shows the state.
+  return (
+    <Pressable
+      accessibilityRole="switch"
+      accessibilityLabel={title}
+      accessibilityState={{ checked: value, disabled }}
+      aria-checked={value}
+      aria-disabled={disabled}
+      disabled={disabled}
+      onPress={() => onValueChange(!value)}
+      style={({ pressed }) => [styles.row, { opacity: disabled ? 0.45 : pressed ? 0.7 : 1 }]}
+    >
+      <View aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants" style={[styles.rowIcon, { backgroundColor: tint }]}>
+        <Icon size={20} strokeWidth={2} color={ink} />
+      </View>
+      <Copy aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no" style={styles.rowTitle}>
+        {title}
+      </Copy>
+      <View pointerEvents="none" aria-hidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
+        <Switch
+          value={value}
+          disabled={disabled}
+          trackColor={{ false: c.line, true: c.aquaDark }}
+          thumbColor={c.paper}
+          ios_backgroundColor={c.line}
+        />
+      </View>
+    </Pressable>
+  );
+}
+
 /** A Row that opens a sheet with longer text (UX4 topic sheet). The text keeps its wording. */
 export function TopicRow({
   title,

### Task 10: The invite-link site for `link.resbite.com`

**Files:**
- Modify: `links/generate.mjs`
- Modify: `links/assets/invite.html`, `links/assets/invite.js`, `links/assets/invite.css`
- Create: `links/generate.test.mjs`
- Modify: `mobile/package.json` (the `test` chain)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `generate(origin: string, teamId: string, bundleId: string, output: string): Promise<string>` and `INVITE_CSP`, exported from `links/generate.mjs`. The command line is unchanged: `node --import ./mobile/node_modules/tsx/dist/loader.mjs links/generate.mjs https://link.resbite.com TEAM_ID com.resbite.app OUTPUT_DIRECTORY`.
- The output folder holds `.well-known/apple-app-site-association`, `invite.html`, `invite.js`, `invite.css`, `resbite-mark.png`, `vercel.json`, `hosting-requirements.txt` and `expo-ios-config.fragment.json`. The owner deploys it as its own Vercel project (see "Rollout").
- `mobile/src/domain/appLinks.test.ts` reads `links/assets/invite.js` and keeps passing: the token rules are unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `links/generate.test.mjs`:

<!-- block: task10-generate-test -->
```js
// The invitation-link site (UX3): Apple's link file is scoped to invitations, and the page and its preview
// never carry event details. Run from mobile/: node --import tsx ../links/generate.test.mjs
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { generate, INVITE_CSP } from "./generate.mjs";

async function site() {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "resbite-links-"));
  await generate("https://go.resbite.com", "ABCDE12345", "com.resbite.app", join(dir, "out"));
  return { out: join(dir, "out"), cleanup: () => rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }) };
}

test("Apple's link file names this app and only the invitation path", async () => {
  const { out, cleanup } = await site();
  try {
    const aasa = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(out, ".well-known/apple-app-site-association"), "utf8"));
    assert.deepEqual(aasa.applinks.details, [
      {
        appIDs: ["ABCDE12345.com.resbite.app"],
        components: [{ "/": "/invite", comment: "Resbite invitations only; auth setup remains separate." }],
      },
    ]);
    const fragment = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(out, "expo-ios-config.fragment.json"), "utf8"));
    assert.deepEqual(fragment, { ios: { associatedDomains: ["applinks:go.resbite.com"] } });
  } finally {
    await cleanup();
  }
});

test("the preview says only “You’re invited to a resbite”, and the page is accessible", async () => {
  const { out, cleanup } = await site();
  try {
    const page = await readFile(join(out, "invite.html"), "utf8");
    assert.match(page, /<meta property="og:title" content="You’re invited to a resbite" \/>/);
    assert.match(page, /<meta property="og:image" content="https:\/\/go\.resbite\.com\/resbite-mark\.png" \/>/);
    assert.ok(!page.includes("{{"), "Every placeholder is filled");
    assert.ok(!/og:description/.test(page), "No description that could carry details");
    assert.match(page, /<html lang="en">/);
    assert.match(page, /<main>/);
    assert.equal(page.match(/<h1>/g)?.length, 1);
    assert.ok(!/maximum-scale|user-scalable/.test(page), "Zoom is never blocked");
    assert.ok(!/https?:\/\/(?!go\.resbite\.com)/.test(page), "No third-party resources");
    const script = await readFile(join(out, "invite.js"), "utf8");
    assert.match(script, /\^#token=\[a-f0-9\]\{64\}\$/);
    assert.match(script, /resbite:\/\/invite\?token=/);
  } finally {
    await cleanup();
  }
});

test("the host serves the link file as JSON and the page with private headers", async () => {
  const { out, cleanup } = await site();
  try {
    const vercel = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(out, "vercel.json"), "utf8"));
    const headers = (source) =>
      Object.fromEntries(vercel.headers.find((h) => h.source === source).headers.map((h) => [h.key, h.value]));
    assert.equal(headers("/.well-known/apple-app-site-association")["Content-Type"], "application/json");
    for (const source of ["/invite", "/invite.html"]) {
      assert.equal(headers(source)["Referrer-Policy"], "no-referrer");
      assert.equal(headers(source)["Cache-Control"], "no-store");
      assert.equal(headers(source)["Content-Security-Policy"], INVITE_CSP);
    }
    assert.deepEqual(vercel.rewrites, [{ source: "/invite", destination: "/invite.html" }]);
  } finally {
    await cleanup();
  }
});

test("a bad team ID or origin is refused before anything is written", async () => {
  await assert.rejects(generate("https://go.resbite.com", "short", "com.resbite.app", "/tmp/never"), /Usage/);
  await assert.rejects(generate("http://go.resbite.com", "ABCDE12345", "com.resbite.app", "/tmp/never"), /HTTPS/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the generator** `links/generate.mjs`:

<!-- block: task10-generate -->
```js
// Generates the invitation-link site for review and deployment (UX3, go.resbite.com). Never uploads anything
// or changes app config: the output folder is deployed separately, as its own Vercel project.
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile, copyFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { resolve, dirname } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { invitationOrigin } from "../mobile/src/domain/appLinks.ts";

/** The headers every invitation page carries; also written into vercel.json so the host serves them. */
export const INVITE_CSP =
  "default-src 'none'; img-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; connect-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'";
const pageHeaders = [
  { key: "Referrer-Policy", value: "no-referrer" },
  { key: "Cache-Control", value: "no-store" },
  { key: "Content-Security-Policy", value: INVITE_CSP },
  { key: "X-Content-Type-Options", value: "nosniff" },
];

export async function generate(originValue, teamId, bundleId, output) {
  if (
    !originValue ||
    !/^[A-Z0-9]{10}$/.test(teamId ?? "") ||
    !/^[A-Za-z0-9-]+(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9-]+)+$/.test(bundleId ?? "") ||
    !output
  ) {
    throw Error(
      "Usage: node --import ./mobile/node_modules/tsx/dist/loader.mjs links/generate.mjs https://OWNED_DOMAIN TEAM_ID BUNDLE_ID OUTPUT_DIRECTORY",
    );
  }
  const origin = invitationOrigin(originValue);
  const root = resolve(output),
    source = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  await mkdir(resolve(root, ".well-known"), { recursive: true });
  const write = (path, data) => writeFile(resolve(root, path), data, { flag: "wx" });
  await write(
    ".well-known/apple-app-site-association",
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        applinks: {
          details: [
            {
              appIDs: [`${teamId}.${bundleId}`],
              components: [{ "/": "/invite", comment: "Resbite invitations only; auth setup remains separate." }],
            },
          ],
        },
      },
      null,
      2,
    ) + "\n",
  );
  // The preview image needs an absolute address; nothing else about the page depends on the origin.
  const page = (await readFile(resolve(source, "assets", "invite.html"), "utf8")).replaceAll("{{ORIGIN}}", origin);
  await write("invite.html", page);
  for (const name of ["invite.js", "invite.css"]) await write(name, await readFile(resolve(source, "assets", name)));
  await copyFile(resolve(source, "../mobile/assets/brand/resbite-mark.png"), resolve(root, "resbite-mark.png"));
  await write(
    "vercel.json",
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        rewrites: [{ source: "/invite", destination: "/invite.html" }],
        redirects: [{ source: "/", destination: "https://www.resbite.com/", permanent: false }],
        headers: [
          {
            source: "/.well-known/apple-app-site-association",
            headers: [
              { key: "Content-Type", value: "application/json" },
              { key: "Cache-Control", value: "max-age=300" },
            ],
          },
          { source: "/invite", headers: pageHeaders },
          { source: "/invite.html", headers: pageHeaders },
        ],
      },
      null,
      2,
    ) + "\n",
  );
  await write(
    "hosting-requirements.txt",
    `Origin: ${origin}\nDeploy this folder as its own Vercel project (vercel.json sets the rewrite and headers).\nServe /invite as invite.html (200, no redirect).\nServe /.well-known/apple-app-site-association as application/json over HTTPS, without authentication or redirects.\nSet Referrer-Policy: no-referrer; Cache-Control: no-store on /invite.\nSet Content-Security-Policy: ${INVITE_CSP}\nDisable request query logging, analytics, session replay and third-party scripts on invitation pages. Fragments are not sent to the server.\nReview before deployment; this script does not publish anything.\n`,
  );
  await write(
    "expo-ios-config.fragment.json",
    JSON.stringify({ ios: { associatedDomains: [`applinks:${new URL(origin).hostname}`] } }, null, 2) + "\n",
  );
  return root;
}

if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) {
  const [originValue, teamId, bundleId, output] = process.argv.slice(2);
  await generate(originValue, teamId, bundleId, output);
  console.log(
    "Generated the invitation-link site. Deploy it as its own Vercel project; domain association, the native build and device checks are still required.",
  );
}
```

`links/assets/invite.js` with:

<!-- block: task10-invite-js -->
```js
"use strict";
(() => {
  const hash = location.hash;
  const hasQuery = Boolean(location.search);
  // Remove the secret from visible history without persisting it anywhere.
  history.replaceState(null, "", "/invite");
  const valid = !hasQuery && /^#token=[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(hash);
  if (!valid) {
    document.getElementById("status").textContent =
      "This invitation link is incomplete. Open the original message again, or ask the person who invited you for a new link.";
    return;
  }
  const open = document.getElementById("open");
  open.href = "resbite://invite?token=" + hash.slice(7);
  open.hidden = false;
})();
```

## Rollout (controller and owner, after the final whole-branch review; not a subagent task)

Each live change waits for the owner's explicit approval in chat. Nothing here runs during Tasks 1–12.

**Order:**
- The coworker's UX2 invitee check on the current TestFlight build comes first.
- The migration and the new build then go live together. Builds older than UX3 read the emptied note column, so they would show no Meeting details.

1. **Link site:**
   - The owner provides the Apple Team ID (`npx eas credentials` or the Apple Developer account shows it).
   - The controller generates the site: `node --import ./mobile/node_modules/tsx/dist/loader.mjs links/generate.mjs https://link.resbite.com TEAM_ID com.resbite.app /tmp/resbite-link`.
   - The owner deploys it as its own Vercel project (from `/tmp/resbite-link`: `npx vercel deploy --prod`, or by uploading in the dashboard), adds the domain `link.resbite.com` to that project, and adds the DNS record Vercel shows (a CNAME for `link`) in Cloudflare, with the proxy off.
   - The controller checks the hosting. `curl -sI https://link.resbite.com/.well-known/apple-app-site-association` must return 200, `application/json` and no redirect. `curl -sI https://link.resbite.com/invite` must return 200 with `no-store`, `no-referrer` and the CSP.
2. **Rehearse again on the final branch:** `./scripts/test-database.sh --hosted-baseline supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`, then record `shasum -a 256 supabase/migrations/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql`.
3. **Read-only before-snapshot** of project `ewcsgvhuojxdpaspwsrx` with MCP `execute_sql`, saved as `qa/ux3-<date>/hosted-before.json`:

```sql
select version, name from supabase_migrations.schema_migrations order by version;
select conname, pg_get_constraintdef(oid) as def from pg_constraint where conrelid='private.notification_outbox'::regclass and contype='c';
select p.oid::regprocedure::text as fn, md5(pg_get_functiondef(p.oid)) as md5 from pg_proc p
 where p.oid in ('private.create_plan_v2(uuid,text,text,text[],text,timestamptz,text,text,text,double precision,double precision)'::regprocedure,
  'private.change_plan_v2(uuid,integer,text,text,text[],timestamptz,text,text,text,boolean)'::regprocedure,
  'private.redact_deleting_owner_plan()'::regprocedure);
select count(*) as plans, count(*) filter (where note<>'') as with_note from public.plans;
select kind, count(*) from private.notification_outbox group by kind;
```

   **Stop if:**
   - the outbox kind check isn't named `notification_outbox_kind_check`;
   - any md5 differs from a fresh local hosted-baseline cluster.
4. **Ask the owner** to approve `ux3_event_details`, quoting the file name, its sha256 and a one-line summary of each slice. No other wording counts as approval.
5. **Apply, when the new build is ready to submit.** On approval, apply with MCP `apply_migration` (name `ux3_event_details`, the file's exact content). Then:
   - take the after-snapshot (the same queries, plus `select count(*) from public.plan_meeting_details`, which must equal the before `with_note`), saved as `hosted-after.json`;
   - run MCP `get_advisors` (security and performance) and expect no new warnings;
   - add `20261015120000_ux3_event_details.sql   <hosted version>` to `supabase/tests/hosted-baseline/migrations.txt`;
   - re-run `./scripts/test-database.sh --hosted-baseline`, now without a candidate, and expect all PASS.
6. **New TestFlight build** (owner):
   - `npx eas env:create production --name EXPO_PUBLIC_INVITATION_ORIGIN --value https://link.resbite.com --visibility plaintext`;
   - `npx eas build --platform ios --profile production`, then `npx eas submit --platform ios --latest`;
   - EAS adds the Associated Domains capability from `ios.associatedDomains`.
7. **Owner's iPhone check** on the new build, using the status doc's list:
   - a `link.resbite.com` invite sent in Messages opens the app; without the app, the fallback page shows;
   - end time;
   - Meeting details, locked then unlocked;
   - Note to guests;
   - Add and Update calendar;
   - the reminder sheet, a real reminder and the switches;
   - Larger Text and VoiceOver on the event page.

   Corroborate each check that leaves a trace with read-only queries.
8. **Then the coworker:** they open a real `link.resbite.com` invite from Messages. Adding testers stays a separate approval each time.
9. **Write `qa/ux3-<date>/README.md`,** then offer the finishing-a-development-branch menu. Push only when the owner says so.

**Rollback:**
- **App:** the previous TestFlight build.
- **Database:** `begin; \i supabase/rollback/20261015120000_ux3_event_details.rollback.sql; commit;` with MCP `execute_sql`, with the owner's approval. It puts every Meeting details text back into `plans.note`. Then remove the `migrations.txt` line. The hosted `schema_migrations` row stays, so any re-application needs a fix-forward migration.
- **Links:** remove the DNS record. The app keeps opening `resbite://` links.
